# ADBuddy M6c · Internacionalización EN/ES · Plan de implementación

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Objetivo:** que ADBuddy hable inglés y español de verdad y que **no se pueda volver a meter texto suelto**: la app Android ya está casi entera en los dos idiomas (se cierran sus huecos y se le ponen controles), y el host Go gana un paquete `i18n` con catálogos, detección de idioma completa (también en Windows) y la traducción de todo lo que ve una persona. Lo que decide el usuario (`rawCommand`, nivel, `special`, el código de la respuesta) no cambia con el idioma; `--json`, los códigos y la salida para agentes siguen en inglés byte a byte.

**Arquitectura:**
- **Host:** `host/internal/i18n` (catálogos TOML embebidos `catalog/en.toml` y `es.toml`, `Lang.T`/`TN`, `Msg` para trozos de frase, `Resolve`/`Detect` y el idioma de Windows) y, en `cli`, `App.Lang`, `app.T`, `NewErrorT` y `tell`. Las razones del clasificador pasan de `string` a `Text{EN, ES}`. Los controles son tests: paridad del catálogo, claves usadas y existentes, razones bilingües, ayuda grabada en inglés, y un contador de literales que solo puede bajar.
- **App:** recursos de plataforma (`values/`, `values-es/`), `ErrorText` por `code`, horas y tamaños según el idioma, `localeConfig`, canales y notificación que se repintan al cambiar de idioma, y dos tests JVM (`StringsParityTest`, `NoHardcodedTextTest`) más lint.
- **Herramientas de apoyo (apéndices A y B, fuera del repo):** `rewrite` (razones del clasificador) e `i18nize` (saca el texto de `host/internal/cli` al catálogo sin cambiar un byte del inglés). Están probadas sobre una réplica del árbol (ver «Verificación del plan»).

**Tecnologías:** Go 1.27, `BurntSushi/toml` y `golang.org/x/sys` (ya están), cobra (ya está; `pflag` pasa de indirecta a directa en `go.mod`), Kotlin/Compose y Gradle 9.8. **Nada nuevo en `go.mod` ni en `libs.versions.toml`.**

**Spec (autoridad):** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-adbuddy-m6c-i18n-design.md` (en la rama `m6c`). Protocolo: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-01-adbuddy-protocolo-m3-m5-design.md` §4.1; M6c **no cambia el protocolo**. Los rulings 1-19 de la spec §12 mandan; donde este plan se aparta de la spec por una medida real, está en «Revisión del plan frente a la spec» y en «Dudas».

**Estado del repositorio al escribir el plan (2026-10-02):** rama `m6c`, sobre `main` 6f51844 (M3, M4, M5a/b, M6a y M6b ya están dentro, y el PR #15, que toca `cli/phone_agent.go`, `cli/phone_perm.go` y `cli/phone_chat_test.go`; M5c todavía no). El plan se escribió sobre 0072dc8 y se corrigió a 6f51844 tras la revisión opus. Ledger: `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-02-adbuddy-m6c-i18n/progress.md`, con cada decisión como `Ruling: qué — por qué — qué cuesta si me equivoco`.

## Restricciones globales

- **Ramas y ficheros ajenos.** Rama `m6c`. No se sube ni se integra en `main` sin el ok del usuario/supervisor; nunca `push --force`; antes de cualquier push, `git grep` de seriales e IPs. **No toques las pantallas de M5c ni sus cadenas** (`protected_*`, `access_*`, `onboarding_*`, `settings_*` de la app son de `nautilus`); M6c solo añade `settings_language*`.
- **Idiomas:** solo `en` y `es`; inglés es la fuente y la caída; el español es completo. Tono (es): tuteo, «móvil» (no «teléfono»), «PC», «agente», «diario», «depuración inalámbrica», «emparejar», «ficheros», sin «usted» ni voseo, sin punto final en botones y rótulos. Órdenes, flags, claves y valores de flags (`adbuddy serve`, `--json`, `ADBUDDY_LANG`, `auto|terminal|deny`) no se traducen nunca.
- **Lo que no se traduce (spec §3, §4.2, §6.3):** `Error.Code`, `Error.Message`/`Fix` en `--json`, claves JSON, `ipc.Exit.ErrorCode`, la salida de los comandos para agentes y de la shim (`screen`, `tap`, `type`, `foreground`, `screenshot`, `send`, `hook`: su ayuda **sí** se traduce, lo que hacen y sus errores **no**), `ADBUDDY_SOURCE=agent`, `Entry.Reason` del diario, mensajes de bloqueo («Blocked by ADBuddy: …»), las plantillas (`AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `NOTES.md`, skills), `ErrorReply.text` (inglés por protocolo), las causas técnicas de paquetes internos (`fmt.Errorf`, errores de adb, red, disco) y lo que viene del agente o del móvil. Las líneas `Run:` de `doctor` son órdenes y no se tocan.
- **Seguridad (spec §4):** la decisión viaja como código; `rawCommand`, `level`, `special`, `expiresAt`, `allowSession` no tienen versión traducida; una traducción incompleta **nunca** esconde información (orden: idioma pedido → inglés, jamás vacío ni el nombre de la clave); `classify.Printable` se aplica **a los argumentos antes de pasarlos a `T`** y el catálogo no lleva marcado ni escapes; un argumento nunca es el patrón de `fmt`. La pantalla de bloqueo no cambia de visibilidad ni gana argumentos. No se toca `mgr.Busy()` ni el reconocimiento de diálogos de Claude Code.
- **El inglés no cambia.** Los tests existentes pasan **sin tocar sus aserciones** (salvo las líneas que cada tarea dice que cambian, y siempre a la versión equivalente en inglés). Por eso `TestMain` de `cli` y de `cmd/adbuddy` fija `ADBUDDY_LANG=en`.
- **Go:** `go` y `gofmt` en el PATH (en esta máquina `"/c/Program Files/Go/bin/go.exe"`). Los tests, siempre con `-count=1` y `-timeout`, **por paquete**; `go test ./internal/cli/` tarda ~85 s y lleva `-timeout 400s`; la suite completa solo al final de cada tarea de host (`-timeout 580s`, en segundo plano si hace falta). **Sin `-race` en Windows** (solo la CI de Linux). Antes de cada commit: `gofmt -l .` vacío y `go vet ./...` con `GOOS=windows`, `linux` y `darwin`.
- **Gradle:** `JAVA_HOME='C:\Users\Jose\AppData\Local\Android\jdk-17.0.20.1+1'` y `/c/Users/Jose/AppData/Local/Gradle/gradle-9.8.0/bin/gradle --no-daemon -q` (en los pasos, `gradle`), desde `android/`. `android/local.properties` no se versiona: si falta, `sdk.dir=C\:/Users/Jose/AppData/Local/Android/Sdk`.
- **Ficheros nuevos y ediciones.** Los ficheros «Crear» llevan el contenido exacto del plan (LF). En «Editar», cada bloque dice el texto que hay y el que lo sustituye; el texto «que hay» aparece una sola vez en el fichero: si no coincide, detente y explícalo. Con la herramienta Edit de siempre.
- **Commits:** identidad local `hybriqtech` (nunca `git config --global`), trailer exacto `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (los implementadores a veces ponen otro: comprobarlo), `git add` con rutas explícitas (nunca `-A`), un commit por tarea (más los de arreglos de revisión).
- **El móvil del usuario no se toca.** Los tests usan el adb falso y, para Android, el emulador (`scripts/emulator.sh`). Lo que necesita el móvil o el PC del usuario va a «Validaciones manuales pendientes».
- **Sin subagentes dentro de una tarea.** Si un paso no sale como dice el plan, **detente y explícalo**.

## Orden, dependencias y árboles

Los árboles `host/` y `android/` son disjuntos: sus tareas pueden ir **a la vez con dos implementadores**; dos implementadores en el mismo árbol, no.

| Tarea | Árbol | Depende de | ¿Espera a otras sesiones? | Implementador | Revisor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **H0** Base del host | `host/` | – | no | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer` |
| **A1** Controles de la app | `android/` | – | no | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer` |
| **A2** Arreglos de la app | `android/` | A1 | no | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` (texto de errores, notificaciones, tarjetas) |
| **H4a** Razones bilingües | `host/` | H0 | no | `adbuddy-implementer`, con **escalado a `adbuddy-implementer-opus`** si se atasca (rondas 4-5 o BLOCKED) | `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` (qué ve el usuario al decidir) |
| **H4b** Confirmación y avisos | `host/` | H0, H4a | no (toca `claudecode/rule.go`: M4 ya está en `main`) | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` |
| **H1** Ayuda de cobra | `host/` | H0 | **M4, M5b y M6a ya están en `main`**: no esperan; sí rebasar antes de empezar | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer` |
| **H2** Errores | `host/` | H0, H1 | ídem | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer` |
| **H3a** `setup`, `appsetup`, `connect` | `host/` | H0-H2 | ídem | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer` |
| **H3b** `doctor` | `host/` | H3a | ídem | **`adbuddy-implementer-opus`**: la única tarea con ~1.000 líneas de ediciones exactas que no se pueden partir; si en la ronda 1 se atasca el implementador por defecto, escalar | `adbuddy-reviewer` (que `Run:` y las órdenes no se traducen) |
| **H3c** el resto de mensajes | `host/` | H3b | ídem | `adbuddy-implementer` | `adbuddy-reviewer` |
| **V** Cierre | ambos | todas | M5c, si ya está en `main` | `adbuddy-implementer` (documentos y comprobaciones) | `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` (revisión final de la rama) |

- **Qué puede ir antes de M4, M5b y M6a** (si alguna vez volviera a hacer falta esperarlas): H0, A1, A2, H4a y H4b (H4b solo toca una línea por función en `daemon.go`, y `claudecode/rule.go`, de M4). **Qué espera a M4, M5b y M6a:** H1, H2 y H3, porque `host/internal/cli/*.go` es el árbol más disputado. Hoy los tres están en `main`; lo que queda abierto es **M5c** (`nautilus`: pantallas de la app, y a lo mejor mensajes del host), y por eso cada tarea de `cli` empieza con `git fetch` y rebase, y el contador de `textlint` (H0) hace visible cualquier literal nuevo de otra sesión.
- **Orden de ejecución:** carril host H0 → H4a → H4b → H1 → H2 → H3a → H3b → H3c; carril Android A1 → A2 en paralelo; V al final.
- **Revisión del plan antes de ejecutarlo (obligatoria, `CLAUDE.md`):** `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` lo compara con la spec antes de la tarea H0 (con atención a §4, §6.5 y el reparto de §3); se corrige con sus hallazgos.
- **Límite de rondas:** 5 por tarea; al llegar, el controlador adjudica. Un hueco que deje ejecutar 🔴 sin confirmación no se aparca nunca. Revisores con tope de tiempo (`go test -timeout`); nunca modelos Haiku. Implementador por defecto: `adbuddy-implementer`; **H3b** (la única tarea con ~1.000 líneas de ediciones exactas, que no se puede partir) va con `adbuddy-implementer-opus` desde el principio, y **H4a** escala a `adbuddy-implementer-opus` si se atasca (rondas 4-5 o BLOCKED); H3c con `adbuddy-implementer`.
- **Cada tarea compila y pasa sus tests por sí sola**; los pasos intermedios de una tarea (por ejemplo «registrar el inglés» antes de «migrar») dejan tests rojos que la misma tarea cierra, y el commit se hace al final.

## Verificación del plan

El plan se ejecutó entero, tarea a tarea, sobre una **réplica** del árbol de `m4` (rama de trabajo fuera del repositorio, un commit por tarea): H0, H4a, H4b, H1, H2, H3a, H3b y H3c con sus catálogos, y A1 y A2 con Gradle (`testDebugUnitTest`, `lintDebug`, `assembleDebug`, `assembleDebugAndroidTest`; 449 tests; el plan corregido añade uno más, el de la pantalla de bloqueo en `StringsParityTest`, que no se ejecutó en la réplica: 450). Para H3c se llevó a la réplica el `envcmd.go` y el `envcmd_test.go` de `main` 0072dc8 (M6b), de modo que `groupSkillPaths` y su test se comprobaron con el código real. Resultados: `go test ./...` verde (31 paquetes) tras H0, H4b, H1 y H3c; `cli` verde tras H2, H3a y H3b; el «inglés idéntico» comprobado con la ayuda grabada, los 260 `Contains` de `cli`, los 42 `TestDoctor*` y el e2e. Después se **repitió el plan entero desde cero** sobre un árbol limpio de `m4`, aplicando solo lo que dice este documento (los ficheros nuevos, las ediciones y las pasadas de `rewrite` e `i18nize`) en el orden H0, H4a, H4b, H1, H2, H3a, H3b, H3c: cada tarea compiló y pasó sus tests por sí sola, y los recuentos de cada pasada (`edits`, `MANUAL`, imports que sobran, filas de `textlint.txt`) son los que dicen los pasos. Cada edición de los pasos «Editar» se aplicó con una herramienta que exige que el texto «que hay» aparezca **una sola vez**, y las listas de claves salen de la pasada real de `i18nize`.

**Base real (revisión opus del plan).** La réplica era de `m4`; la base de la rama `m6c` es `main` **6f51844**. Entre 0072dc8 y 6f51844 solo cambiaron `cli/phone_agent.go` (`afterStop(in, delay)`, `tailLoop(…, poll, grace)`, `pollTranscript(…, grace)`: los tiempos se leen antes de lanzar la goroutine), `cli/phone_perm.go` (`timeout := permTimeout`, `ExpiresAt: now.Add(timeout).UTC()`) y `cli/phone_chat_test.go` (helper `processed`). Los bloques «lo que hay» de H4b (`phone_agent.go`, `phone_perm.go`) y de H3c (`phone_agent.go`) se comprobaron contra el árbol de 6f51844 y coinciden; ninguna edición del plan toca esas firmas.

**No se replicó** (y el implementador lo valida con las puertas de su tarea): el árbol completo de `m6c` (solo `envcmd.go`; el resto de M6b no toca los ficheros de estas tareas); la ejecución de `LocalesTest` en un emulador (solo se compila); el comportamiento en Windows en español (la detección de `GetUserPreferredUILanguages` se prueba con tablas, no con un Windows en español); y el rebase contra M5c.

## Tarea H0: Base del host (`i18n`, detección, `App.Lang`, controles activos)

**Árbol:** `host/` · **Revisor:** `adbuddy-reviewer` · **Commit:** `feat(host): paquete i18n, detección de idioma y controles de catálogo`

**Ficheros:**
- Crear: `host/internal/i18n/{i18n.go,detect.go,detect_windows.go,detect_other.go}`, `host/internal/i18n/catalog/{en.toml,es.toml}`, `host/internal/i18n/i18ntest/{i18ntest.go,noenglish.go}`, tests `i18n_test.go`, `detect_test.go`, `catalog_test.go`, `i18ntest/noenglish_test.go`.
- Crear: `host/internal/cli/{lang.go,console_windows.go,console_other.go,lang_test.go,textlint_test.go}` y `host/internal/cli/testdata/textlint.txt`.
- Modificar: `host/internal/config/config.go` (+ test), `host/internal/cli/root.go`, `host/internal/cli/main_test.go` (una línea), `host/cmd/adbuddy/e2e_test.go` (una línea; fichero de M6a/M3: commit **aparte** de pocas líneas), `.gitattributes`.

**Interfaces que produce (las usan todas las tareas siguientes):**
- `i18n.Lang` (`EN`, `ES`; el valor cero es inglés) con `Norm()`, `T(key, args...) string`, `TN(key, n, args...) string`; `i18n.Msg`/`i18n.M(key, args...)` (un mensaje sin escribir: como argumento de `T` sale en el idioma de esa `T`; `String()` es su inglés); `i18n.Parse(string) Lang`, `Resolve(getenv, configLang) Lang` (puro), `Detect(getenv, configLang, system func() string) Lang`, `SystemLanguage() string`; `i18n.Has`, `i18n.Pairs()`, `i18n.WithoutKeys(lang, keys...)` (para tests).
- `i18ntest.Lang(t, lang)` (`ADBUDDY_LANG` para el test), `i18ntest.Without(t, lang, keys...)`, `i18ntest.NoEnglish(t, out, exceptKeys...)`, `i18ntest.Problems(out, pairs, except...)`.
- `config.Config.Lang(getenv) string` (delega en `Resolve`), `config.ReadLanguage(path) string`.
- `cli.App.Lang i18n.Lang`; `(*App).T/TN/Log`; `tell(w io.Writer, key, args...)` y `langWriter` (en `say.go`, H3a); `detectLang(getenv)`, `withLang(getenv, lang)`, `utf8Console(files...)`.
- Los tests del paquete `i18n` son los controles: `TestCatalogParity` (mismas claves, mismos `%[n]verbo`, sin vacíos, `.one`/`.other` en pareja, nada igual en en y es salvo `sameInBoth`), `TestKeysExist` (analizador `go/ast`: toda clave usada con `T`, `TN`, `NewErrorT`/`ConfigErrorT`/`TransientErrorT`/`UsageErrorT`, `i18n.M`, `tell`, `say`, `warn` existe en `en.toml`, con tantos argumentos como usa, y ninguna clave sobra; `keyTables` y `dynamicPrefixes` declaran las pocas excepciones, cada una con su motivo).
- `cli/textlint_test.go` cuenta los literales de texto que quedan en `internal/cli` por fichero y regla (`cobra`, `flag`, `error`, `print`, `check`) y **solo pueden bajar**: uno nuevo falla, y un recuento menor que el de `testdata/textlint.txt` también falla (se baja con `-update-lint` y se lee el diff). `exemptions` son los ficheros cuyo texto no es para personas, cada uno con su motivo.

- [ ] **Paso 1: Rama y base.** `git switch m6c` (ya existe); `git fetch` y comprueba que está sobre `main` actual. Crea el ledger `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-02-adbuddy-m6c-i18n/progress.md` con la cabecera (spec, plan, fecha).

- [ ] **Paso 2: Crear los ficheros** (los tests son los de la tarea: fallarán a compilar hasta el paso 3, y eso es el rojo de TDD).

**Crear `host/internal/cli/console_other.go`:**

```go
//go:build !windows

package cli

import "os"

// utf8Console does nothing outside Windows: terminals are UTF-8 already.
func utf8Console(...*os.File) func() { return func() {} }
```

**Crear `host/internal/cli/console_windows.go`:**

```go
package cli

import (
	"os"

	"golang.org/x/sys/windows"
)

// cpUTF8 is the code page of UTF-8.
const cpUTF8 = 65001

// utf8Console puts the output code page of the console on UTF-8 until the
// returned function runs, when one of files is a console: ADBuddy writes
// UTF-8 (accents, ñ, the emoji of the journal) and an old console (code page
// 850 or 1252) would draw it wrong. With no console, or when it already is
// UTF-8, it does nothing.
func utf8Console(files ...*os.File) func() {
	console := false
	for _, f := range files {
		var mode uint32
		if f != nil && windows.GetConsoleMode(windows.Handle(f.Fd()), &mode) == nil {
			console = true
		}
	}
	if !console {
		return func() {}
	}
	old, err := windows.GetConsoleOutputCP()
	if err != nil || old == cpUTF8 {
		return func() {}
	}
	if windows.SetConsoleOutputCP(cpUTF8) != nil {
		return func() {}
	}
	return func() { _ = windows.SetConsoleOutputCP(old) }
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/cli/lang.go`:**

```go
package cli

import (
	"io"
	"os"

	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/config"
	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/i18n"
	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/paths"
)

// T is the message key (catalog/en.toml and es.toml) in the language this run
// speaks. See speaks for when that is English whatever the person's language.
func (a *App) T(key string, args ...any) string { return a.speaks().T(key, args...) }

// TN is T for a message that depends on a count (key.one and key.other).
func (a *App) TN(key string, n int, args ...any) string { return a.speaks().TN(key, n, args...) }

// speaks is the language of what this run writes for people: App.Lang, except
// in English when the output is for a program: --json, or a command run by the
// agent (ADBUDDY_SOURCE=agent), whose reader is a model that works from the
// English text. An App built by hand has no Lang, which is English.
func (a *App) speaks() i18n.Lang {
	if a.JSON || (a.Getenv != nil && a.Getenv("ADBUDDY_SOURCE") == "agent") {
		return i18n.EN
	}
	return a.Lang.Norm()
}

// detectLang is the language of this run: ADBUDDY_LANG, the "language" of
// config.toml (read leniently: a broken file does not stop --help), the POSIX
// variables, and then, in Windows, the interface language of the user.
func detectLang(getenv func(string) string) i18n.Lang {
	cfg := ""
	if dirs, err := paths.Default(getenv); err == nil {
		cfg = config.ReadLanguage(dirs.ConfigFile())
	}
	return i18n.Detect(getenv, cfg, i18n.SystemLanguage)
}

// withLang is getenv, but ADBUDDY_LANG answers lang: whoever asks the App
// for the language again (config.Config.Lang in serve, which hands it to the
// daemon and to `remote on`) gets the one this run already decided, the
// Windows interface language included, so no two parts disagree.
func withLang(getenv func(string) string, lang i18n.Lang) func(string) string {
	return func(k string) string {
		if k == "ADBUDDY_LANG" {
			return string(lang)
		}
		return getenv(k)
	}
}

func asFile(w io.Writer) *os.File {
	f, _ := w.(*os.File)
	return f
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/cli/lang_test.go`:**

```go
package cli

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"

	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/config"
	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/i18n"
	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/i18n/i18ntest"
)

func envOf(m map[string]string) func(string) string { return func(k string) string { return m[k] } }

func TestTestMainPinsEnglish(t *testing.T) {
	// The tests that existed before M6c read English text: the TestMain of this
	// package fixes ADBUDDY_LANG=en, which beats config.language, LANG and the
	// language of Windows, so the machine the tests run on never matters.
	if got := os.Getenv("ADBUDDY_LANG"); got != "en" {
		t.Fatalf("ADBUDDY_LANG = %q: TestMain must set it to en", got)
	}
	i18ntest.Lang(t, i18n.ES)
	if got := os.Getenv("ADBUDDY_LANG"); got != "es" {
		t.Fatalf("i18ntest.Lang did not set it: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestSpeaks(t *testing.T) {
	for _, c := range []struct {
		name string
		app  App
		want i18n.Lang
	}{
		{"built by hand: English", App{}, i18n.EN},
		{"es", App{Lang: i18n.ES, Getenv: envOf(nil)}, i18n.ES},
		{"es with --json is English", App{Lang: i18n.ES, JSON: true, Getenv: envOf(nil)}, i18n.EN},
		{"es for the agent is English", App{Lang: i18n.ES, Getenv: envOf(map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_SOURCE": "agent"})}, i18n.EN},
		{"es for the user stays es", App{Lang: i18n.ES, Getenv: envOf(map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_SOURCE": "user"})}, i18n.ES},
		{"no Getenv does not panic", App{Lang: i18n.ES}, i18n.ES},
	} {
		if got := c.app.speaks(); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: %q, want %q", c.name, got, c.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestDetectLang(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	write := func(content string) {
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "config.toml"), []byte(content), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	env := func(extra map[string]string) func(string) string {
		m := map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_CONFIG_DIR": dir, "ADBUDDY_DATA_DIR": dir}
		for k, v := range extra {
			m[k] = v
		}
		return envOf(m)
	}
	write("language = \"es\"\n")
	if got := detectLang(env(nil)); got != i18n.ES {
		t.Errorf("config.language es: %q", got)
	}
	if got := detectLang(env(map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "en"})); got != i18n.EN {
		t.Errorf("ADBUDDY_LANG must win over config.language: %q", got)
	}
	write("language = \"en\"\n")
	if got := detectLang(env(map[string]string{"LANG": "es_ES.UTF-8"})); got != i18n.EN {
		t.Errorf("config.language en must win over LANG: %q", got)
	}
	write("this is [not toml")
	if got := detectLang(env(map[string]string{"LANG": "es_ES.UTF-8"})); got != i18n.ES {
		t.Errorf("a broken config.toml is ignored, LANG decides: %q", got)
	}
	write("typo = 1\nlanguage = \"es\"\n")
	if got := detectLang(env(nil)); got != i18n.ES {
		t.Errorf("other keys do not stop language from being read: %q", got)
	}
	if err := os.Remove(filepath.Join(dir, "config.toml")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := detectLang(env(map[string]string{"LC_ALL": "C", "LANG": "es_ES"})); got != i18n.EN {
		t.Errorf("no config, LC_ALL=C: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestWithLangAgreesWithConfigLang(t *testing.T) {
	// serve asks config.Config.Lang(app.Getenv): it must get the language the
	// run decided, also when it came from the system and not from the variables.
	getenv := withLang(envOf(map[string]string{"LANG": "en_US"}), i18n.ES)
	if got := config.Default().Lang(getenv); got != "es" {
		t.Errorf("Config.Lang = %q, want es", got)
	}
	if got := getenv("LANG"); got != "en_US" {
		t.Errorf("the other variables pass through: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestUTF8ConsoleDoesNothingWithoutAConsole(t *testing.T) {
	utf8Console()()
	utf8Console(nil)()
	f, err := os.CreateTemp(t.TempDir(), "out")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer f.Close()
	utf8Console(f, nil)()
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/cli/textlint_test.go`:**

```go
package cli

import (
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// The text that a person reads comes from the catalog (app.T, NewErrorT…), not
// from a literal in the code. This test finds the literals that are left in
// internal/cli, by rule:
//
//	cobra   the Short or Long of a cobra.Command
//	flag    the help of a flag
//	error   the message or the fix of NewError, ConfigError, TransientError, UsageError
//	print   the text of fmt.Fprint, Fprintf or Fprintln, and of the say and warn helpers of setup
//	check   the Detail or Guidance of a doctor check
//
// Examples are command lines and are not text. What is left is counted per
// file and rule in testdata/textlint.txt and can only go down: a new literal
// fails the test, and so does a count that is lower than the file says (lower
// it with -update-lint, and read the diff). exemptions are the files whose
// text is not for a person (it is for the agent, or it is a log), each with
// its reason.
var updateLint = flag.Bool("update-lint", false, "rewrite testdata/textlint.txt")

// exemptions: file -> rule -> why that text stays in English and in the code.
var exemptions = map[string]map[string]string{}

var wordRE = regexp.MustCompile(`\p{L}{2,}`)

// hasText reports whether e is, or contains, a literal with a word in it: a
// string, a concatenation, or the format of a fmt.Sprintf.
func hasText(e ast.Expr) bool {
	switch v := e.(type) {
	case *ast.BasicLit:
		if v.Kind != token.STRING {
			return false
		}
		s, err := strconv.Unquote(v.Value)
		return err == nil && wordRE.MatchString(s)
	case *ast.BinaryExpr:
		return v.Op == token.ADD && (hasText(v.X) || hasText(v.Y))
	case *ast.ParenExpr:
		return hasText(v.X)
	case *ast.CallExpr:
		if sel, ok := v.Fun.(*ast.SelectorExpr); ok && sel.Sel.Name == "Sprintf" && len(v.Args) > 0 {
			return hasText(v.Args[0])
		}
	}
	return false
}

// hasSpace reports whether a literal of e (a string or a concatenation) has a space.
func hasSpace(e ast.Expr) bool {
	switch v := e.(type) {
	case *ast.BasicLit:
		return v.Kind == token.STRING && strings.Contains(v.Value, " ")
	case *ast.BinaryExpr:
		return v.Op == token.ADD && (hasSpace(v.X) || hasSpace(v.Y))
	case *ast.ParenExpr:
		return hasSpace(v.X)
	}
	return false
}

var flagFuncs = map[string]bool{
	"String": true, "StringVar": true, "StringP": true, "StringVarP": true, "Bool": true, "BoolVar": true, "BoolP": true, "BoolVarP": true,
	"Int": true, "IntVar": true, "IntP": true, "IntVarP": true, "Duration": true, "DurationVar": true, "DurationP": true,
	"StringSlice": true, "StringSliceVar": true, "StringArray": true, "StringArrayVar": true, "Float64": true, "Float64Var": true,
}

type lintHit struct {
	file, rule string
	line       int
}

// lintFile finds the literals of one file.
func lintFile(fset *token.FileSet, name string, f *ast.File) []lintHit {
	var hits []lintHit
	add := func(rule string, p token.Pos) { hits = append(hits, lintHit{name, rule, fset.Position(p).Line}) }
	ast.Inspect(f, func(n ast.Node) bool {
		switch v := n.(type) {
		case *ast.KeyValueExpr:
			if id, ok := v.Key.(*ast.Ident); ok && hasText(v.Value) {
				switch id.Name {
				case "Short", "Long":
					add("cobra", v.Pos())
				case "Detail", "Guidance":
					add("check", v.Pos())
				}
			}
		case *ast.CallExpr:
			var recv ast.Expr
			fname := ""
			switch fn := v.Fun.(type) {
			case *ast.Ident:
				fname = fn.Name
			case *ast.SelectorExpr:
				fname, recv = fn.Sel.Name, fn.X
			}
			switch {
			case fname == "NewError" || fname == "ConfigError" || fname == "TransientError":
				if len(v.Args) == 3 && (hasText(v.Args[1]) || hasText(v.Args[2])) {
					add("error", v.Pos())
				}
			case fname == "UsageError":
				if len(v.Args) == 1 && hasText(v.Args[0]) {
					add("error", v.Pos())
				}
			case (fname == "Fprint" || fname == "Fprintf" || fname == "Fprintln") && recv != nil:
				if len(v.Args) >= 2 && hasText(v.Args[1]) {
					add("print", v.Pos())
				}
			case (fname == "say" || fname == "warn") && recv == nil:
				// A key (setup.getting_tools) has no space: only a phrase is text.
				if len(v.Args) >= 1 && hasText(v.Args[0]) && hasSpace(v.Args[0]) {
					add("print", v.Pos())
				}
			case flagFuncs[fname] && recv != nil && len(v.Args) > 0:
				if c, ok := recv.(*ast.CallExpr); ok {
					if s, ok := c.Fun.(*ast.SelectorExpr); ok && (s.Sel.Name == "Flags" || s.Sel.Name == "PersistentFlags") && hasText(v.Args[len(v.Args)-1]) {
						add("flag", v.Pos())
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return true
	})
	return hits
}

// lintCounts is the literals of the Go files of dir per file and rule, without
// the exempt ones.
func lintCounts(t *testing.T, dir string) map[string]int {
	t.Helper()
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	names, err := filepath.Glob(filepath.Join(dir, "*.go"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out := map[string]int{}
	for _, path := range names {
		if strings.HasSuffix(path, "_test.go") {
			continue
		}
		f, err := parser.ParseFile(fset, path, nil, 0)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		base := filepath.Base(path)
		for _, h := range lintFile(fset, base, f) {
			if exemptions[base][h.rule] != "" {
				continue
			}
			out[base+" "+h.rule]++
		}
	}
	return out
}

func formatLint(c map[string]int) string {
	keys := make([]string, 0, len(c))
	for k := range c {
		keys = append(keys, k)
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("# Text literals left in internal/cli, per file and rule (textlint_test.go). It only goes down.\n")
	for _, k := range keys {
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "%s %d\n", k, c[k])
	}
	return b.String()
}

func readLint(t *testing.T) map[string]int {
	t.Helper()
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join("testdata", "textlint.txt"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out := map[string]int{}
	for _, line := range strings.Split(string(data), "\n") {
		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
		if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
			continue
		}
		parts := strings.Fields(line)
		if len(parts) != 3 {
			t.Fatalf("textlint.txt: %q", line)
		}
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(parts[2])
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("textlint.txt: %q", line)
		}
		out[parts[0]+" "+parts[1]] = n
	}
	return out
}

func TestNoTextLeftInTheCode(t *testing.T) {
	got := lintCounts(t, ".")
	if *updateLint {
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join("testdata", "textlint.txt"), []byte(formatLint(got)), 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return
	}
	want := readLint(t)
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	var keys []string
	for k := range got {
		seen[k] = true
		keys = append(keys, k)
	}
	for k := range want {
		if !seen[k] {
			keys = append(keys, k)
		}
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)
	for _, k := range keys {
		switch g, w := got[k], want[k]; {
		case g > w:
			t.Errorf("%s: %d literals and %d recorded: text for a person goes in catalog/en.toml and es.toml and is used with app.T or NewErrorT (see host/README.md)", k, g, w)
		case g < w:
			t.Errorf("%s: %d literals left and %d recorded: well done, lower it (go test ./internal/cli -run TestNoTextLeftInTheCode -update-lint)", k, g, w)
		}
	}
}

func TestTextLintSeesWhatItShould(t *testing.T) {
	src := `package p
func f(app *App) {
	_ = &cobra.Command{Short: "Do the thing", Long: app.T("x.long"), Example: "  adbuddy x"}
	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&fix, "fix", false, "also restart the server")
	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&fix, "ok", false, app.T("x.flag.ok"))
	_ = NewError("C", "went wrong", "")
	_ = NewError("C", err.Error(), "Check the file.")
	_ = NewError("C", err.Error(), "")
	_ = NewErrorT("C", "x.msg", "x.fix")
	_ = UsageError("needs a number")
	_ = UsageError(err.Error())
	fmt.Fprintln(app.Stderr, "Now doing it")
	fmt.Fprintf(app.Stdout, "%s · %d\n", a, b)
	fmt.Fprintf(w, "done in %s\n", d)
	say("Looking for your phone…\n")
	say("%s", prompt)
	warn(app.T("x.warn"))
	fmt.Fprintln(app.Stderr, app.T("x.say"))
	_ = check{Name: "adb", Detail: "not installed", Guidance: app.T("x.g")}
}`
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	f, err := parser.ParseFile(fset, "p.go", src, 0)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got := map[string]int{}
	for _, h := range lintFile(fset, "p.go", f) {
		got[h.rule]++
	}
	want := map[string]int{"cobra": 1, "flag": 1, "error": 3, "print": 3, "check": 1}
	for k, v := range want {
		if got[k] != v {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d, want %d", k, got[k], v)
		}
	}
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/catalog/es.toml`:**

```toml
# The messages of the host in Spanish: the same keys as en.toml, the same verbs (see host/README.md).
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/catalog_test.go`:**

```go
package i18n

import (
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// sameInBoth are the keys whose text is the same in English and Spanish on
// purpose (a proper name, a command, a symbol). Each one says why.
var sameInBoth = map[string]string{}

// keyTables are the variables that hold catalog keys by a code (a map from an
// error code to the key of its fix): their string values count as used keys,
// and a call may take its key from them (`NewErrorT(code, msg, fixKeys[code])`).
// Each one says where it is.
var keyTables = map[string]string{}

// dynamicPrefixes are the key prefixes that a call builds at run time
// (`T("doctor.name."+id)`): every catalog key with one of them counts as used,
// and the call is accepted without a literal key. Each one says where it is built.
var dynamicPrefixes = map[string]string{}

// verbs lists the fmt verbs of a catalog pattern as "index:verb", and what is
// wrong with it. Every verb needs an explicit index (%[1]s), so that Spanish
// may reorder them and a pattern may use fewer arguments than its call gives.
func verbs(pattern string) (found []string, problems []string) {
	next := 1
	explicit, implicit := 0, 0
	for i := 0; i < len(pattern); i++ {
		if pattern[i] != '%' {
			continue
		}
		at := i
		i++
		if i >= len(pattern) {
			problems = append(problems, "a lone % at the end")
			break
		}
		if pattern[i] == '%' {
			continue
		}
		idx := 0
		if pattern[i] == '[' {
			end := strings.IndexByte(pattern[i:], ']')
			if end < 0 {
				problems = append(problems, "an unclosed [")
				break
			}
			n, err := strconv.Atoi(pattern[i+1 : i+end])
			if err != nil || n < 1 {
				problems = append(problems, "a bad index "+pattern[i:i+end+1])
				break
			}
			idx = n
			i += end + 1
			explicit++
		} else {
			idx = next
			implicit++
		}
		for i < len(pattern) && strings.IndexByte("+-# 0123456789.", pattern[i]) >= 0 {
			i++
		}
		if i >= len(pattern) || strings.IndexByte("sdvqxXtf", pattern[i]) < 0 {
			problems = append(problems, fmt.Sprintf("an unsupported verb at %q", pattern[at:min(i+1, len(pattern))]))
			break
		}
		found = append(found, fmt.Sprintf("%d:%c", idx, pattern[i]))
		next = idx + 1
	}
	if implicit > 0 {
		problems = append(problems, "every verb needs an explicit index (%[1]s, %[2]s)")
	}
	sort.Strings(found)
	return found, problems
}

var letters = regexp.MustCompile(`[\p{L}]{2,}`)

func contains(l []string, s string) bool {
	for _, x := range l {
		if x == s {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// parityProblems compares two catalogs: same keys, no empty text, the same
// verbs with the same index in each pair, plural forms in pairs that use the
// count, and no text copied untranslated.
func parityProblems(en, es map[string]string) []string {
	var out []string
	add := func(f string, a ...any) { out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf(f, a...)) }
	for k, e := range en {
		s, ok := es[k]
		if !ok {
			add("%s: missing in es.toml", k)
			continue
		}
		if strings.TrimSpace(e) == "" {
			add("%s: empty in en.toml", k)
		}
		if strings.TrimSpace(s) == "" {
			add("%s: empty in es.toml", k)
		}
		ve, pe := verbs(e)
		vs, ps := verbs(s)
		for _, p := range pe {
			add("%s (en): %s", k, p)
		}
		for _, p := range ps {
			add("%s (es): %s", k, p)
		}
		if strings.Join(ve, " ") != strings.Join(vs, " ") {
			add("%s: the verbs differ: en %v, es %v", k, ve, vs)
		}
		if e == s && letters.MatchString(e) && sameInBoth[k] == "" {
			add("%s: the same text in en and es (untranslated?); list it in sameInBoth with a reason if it is right", k)
		}
	}
	for k := range es {
		if _, ok := en[k]; !ok {
			add("%s: in es.toml but not in en.toml (English is the source)", k)
		}
	}
	for k := range en {
		if base, ok := strings.CutSuffix(k, ".one"); ok {
			if _, ok := en[base+".other"]; !ok {
				add("%s: a .one without its .other", k)
			}
		}
		if base, ok := strings.CutSuffix(k, ".other"); ok {
			if _, ok := en[base+".one"]; !ok {
				add("%s: a .other without its .one", k)
			}
		}
		if strings.HasSuffix(k, ".one") || strings.HasSuffix(k, ".other") {
			for _, l := range []map[string]string{en, es} {
				if v, _ := verbs(l[k]); !contains(v, "1:d") {
					add("%s: a plural form must show the count with %%[1]d", k)
					break
				}
			}
		}
	}
	sort.Strings(out)
	return out
}

func readCatalog(t *testing.T, lang string) map[string]string {
	t.Helper()
	data, err := catalogFS.ReadFile("catalog/" + lang + ".toml")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m, err := parseCatalog(data)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s.toml: %v", lang, err)
	}
	return m
}

func TestCatalogParity(t *testing.T) {
	for _, p := range parityProblems(readCatalog(t, "en"), readCatalog(t, "es")) {
		t.Error(p)
	}
}

func TestParityAnalyzer(t *testing.T) {
	good := map[string]string{"a": "Hello %[1]s", "b.one": "%[1]d file", "b.other": "%[1]d files", "c": "100%% of %[1]s in %[2]s"}
	goodES := map[string]string{"a": "Hola %[1]s", "b.one": "%[1]d fichero", "b.other": "%[1]d ficheros", "c": "100%% de %[1]s en %[2]s"}
	if p := parityProblems(good, goodES); len(p) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a good pair has problems: %v", p)
	}
	bad := func(en, es map[string]string, want string) {
		t.Helper()
		for _, p := range parityProblems(en, es) {
			if strings.Contains(p, want) {
				return
			}
		}
		t.Errorf("no problem containing %q for %v / %v: %v", want, en, es, parityProblems(en, es))
	}
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "x"}, map[string]string{}, "missing in es.toml")
	bad(map[string]string{}, map[string]string{"a": "x"}, "not in en.toml")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "Hello"}, map[string]string{"a": " "}, "empty in es.toml")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "Hello %[1]s"}, map[string]string{"a": "Hola %[1]d"}, "verbs differ")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "Hello %[1]s"}, map[string]string{"a": "Hola"}, "verbs differ")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "%[1]s of %[2]s"}, map[string]string{"a": "%[2]s de %[1]d"}, "verbs differ")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "%s of %s"}, map[string]string{"a": "%s de %s"}, "explicit index")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "Hello %s"}, map[string]string{"a": "Hola %s"}, "explicit index")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "100%!"}, map[string]string{"a": "100%!"}, "unsupported verb")
	bad(map[string]string{"a": "Same words"}, map[string]string{"a": "Same words"}, "untranslated")
	bad(map[string]string{"a.one": "%[1]d x"}, map[string]string{"a.one": "%[1]d y"}, "without its .other")
	bad(map[string]string{"a.one": "one", "a.other": "%[1]d x"}, map[string]string{"a.one": "uno", "a.other": "%[1]d y"}, "show the count")
	// The same verbs in another order are fine: Spanish may reorder.
	if p := parityProblems(map[string]string{"a": "%[1]s of %[2]s"}, map[string]string{"a": "%[2]s de %[1]s"}); len(p) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("reordering is allowed: %v", p)
	}
}

// ---- every key the code uses exists, and every key is used ----

type use struct {
	key   string
	args  int // the arguments the call passes after the key; -1 when it spreads a slice
	where string
}

// callSpec says where a function takes catalog keys: the positions of its key
// arguments and where the arguments of the pattern start (-1: unknown).
type callSpec struct {
	keys     []int
	argStart int
	method   bool // only as a method call (lang.T, app.T): a bare T(...) is something else
}

var callSpecs = map[string]callSpec{
	"T":               {keys: []int{0}, argStart: 1, method: true},
	"TN":              {keys: []int{0}, argStart: 1, method: true}, // the count is the first argument of the pattern
	"NewErrorT":       {keys: []int{1, 2}, argStart: 3},
	"ConfigErrorT":    {keys: []int{1, 2}, argStart: 3},
	"TransientErrorT": {keys: []int{1, 2}, argStart: 3},
	"UsageErrorT":     {keys: []int{0}, argStart: 1},
	"errorT":          {keys: []int{3, 4}, argStart: -1},           // cli/errors.go: the shared builder
	"M":               {keys: []int{0}, argStart: 1, method: true}, // i18n.M: a message as an argument of another
	"tell":            {keys: []int{1}, argStart: 2},               // cli/say.go: tell(w, key, args...)
}

// keyUses finds the catalog keys a Go source asks for (see callSpecs). A key
// that is a parameter of the enclosing function, or a field of its receiver or
// of a parameter, is a forwarder (func (a *App) T(key string, …) { …T(key, …) })
// and is skipped. Any other key that is not a literal, and does not start
// with a dynamic prefix, is reported in dynamic.
func keyUses(fset *token.FileSet, f *ast.File) (uses []use, dynamic []string) {
	for _, d := range f.Decls {
		params := map[string]bool{}
		if fd, ok := d.(*ast.FuncDecl); ok {
			lists := []*ast.FieldList{fd.Type.Params}
			if fd.Recv != nil {
				lists = append(lists, fd.Recv)
			}
			for _, l := range lists {
				for _, fl := range l.List {
					for _, n := range fl.Names {
						params[n.Name] = true
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ast.Inspect(d, func(n ast.Node) bool { // the parameters of the closures count too: say := func(key string, …)
			if fl, ok := n.(*ast.FuncLit); ok && fl.Type.Params != nil {
				for _, f := range fl.Type.Params.List {
					for _, name := range f.Names {
						params[name.Name] = true
					}
				}
			}
			return true
		})
		ast.Inspect(d, func(n ast.Node) bool {
			c, ok := n.(*ast.CallExpr)
			if !ok {
				return true
			}
			name, method := "", false
			switch fn := c.Fun.(type) {
			case *ast.SelectorExpr:
				name, method = fn.Sel.Name, true
			case *ast.Ident:
				name = fn.Name
			}
			spec, ok := callSpecs[name]
			if !ok || (spec.method && !method) {
				return true
			}
			pos := fset.Position(c.Pos()).String()
			args := -1
			if spec.argStart >= 0 && c.Ellipsis == token.NoPos {
				args = len(c.Args) - spec.argStart
			}
			for _, i := range spec.keys {
				if i >= len(c.Args) || (spec.argStart >= 0 && len(c.Args) <= spec.argStart-1) {
					continue
				}
				e := c.Args[i]
				if lit, ok := e.(*ast.BasicLit); ok && lit.Kind == token.STRING {
					if k, err := strconv.Unquote(lit.Value); err == nil && k != "" {
						uses = append(uses, use{k, args, pos})
					}
					continue
				}
				if forwarded(e, params) {
					continue
				}
				if b, ok := e.(*ast.BinaryExpr); ok && b.Op == token.ADD {
					if lit, ok := b.X.(*ast.BasicLit); ok && lit.Kind == token.STRING {
						if p, _ := strconv.Unquote(lit.Value); dynamicPrefixes[p] != "" {
							continue
						}
					}
				}
				dynamic = append(dynamic, pos)
			}
			return true
		})
	}
	return uses, dynamic
}

// forwarded: e is a parameter of the enclosing function, or a field of one, or
// an element of a key table.
func forwarded(e ast.Expr, params map[string]bool) bool {
	if ix, ok := e.(*ast.IndexExpr); ok {
		if id, ok := ix.X.(*ast.Ident); ok && keyTables[id.Name] != "" {
			return true
		}
	}
	for {
		switch v := e.(type) {
		case *ast.Ident:
			return params[v.Name]
		case *ast.SelectorExpr:
			e = v.X
		default:
			return false
		}
	}
}

// tableUses are the keys of the key tables declared in f: the string values
// of `var <table> = map[string]string{…}`.
func tableUses(fset *token.FileSet, f *ast.File) (uses []use) {
	for _, d := range f.Decls {
		gd, ok := d.(*ast.GenDecl)
		if !ok || gd.Tok != token.VAR {
			continue
		}
		for _, s := range gd.Specs {
			vs, ok := s.(*ast.ValueSpec)
			if !ok || len(vs.Names) != 1 || keyTables[vs.Names[0].Name] == "" || len(vs.Values) != 1 {
				continue
			}
			if cl, ok := vs.Values[0].(*ast.CompositeLit); ok {
				for _, el := range cl.Elts {
					if kv, ok := el.(*ast.KeyValueExpr); ok {
						if lit, ok := kv.Value.(*ast.BasicLit); ok && lit.Kind == token.STRING {
							if k, err := strconv.Unquote(lit.Value); err == nil {
								uses = append(uses, use{k, -1, fset.Position(lit.Pos()).String()})
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return uses
}

func TestKeyUsesAnalyzer(t *testing.T) {
	src := `package p
func f(app *App, lang Lang) {
	_ = app.T("a.one")
	_ = lang.TN("a.n", 3, "x")
	_ = NewErrorT("C", "e.msg", "e.fix", 1, 2)
	_ = NewErrorT("C", "e.msg2", "", 1)
	_ = ConfigErrorT("C", "e.cfg", "e.cfgfix")
	_ = TransientErrorT("C", "e.tr", "e.trfix", 1)
	_ = UsageErrorT("e.use", 1)
	_ = i18n.M("m.msg", 1)
	tell(w, "t.say", 1, 2)
	_ = app.T("a.spread", xs...)
	_ = app.T("dyn." + id)
	_ = app.T(key)
	_ = other.T(key, args...)
	T("not.a.method")
}

var fixKeys = map[string]string{"A": "t.a", "B": "t.b"}

func g(code string) error { return NewErrorT(code, "e.table", fixKeys[code], 1) }

func (e *Error) in(lang Lang, key string) string {
	_ = lang.T(e.key, e.args...)
	_ = lang.T(key, e.args...)
	_ = lang.T(key)
	return errorT(1, false, "C", key, "e.shared", args)
}`
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	f, err := parser.ParseFile(fset, "p.go", src, 0)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	old, oldTables := dynamicPrefixes, keyTables
	dynamicPrefixes = map[string]string{"dyn.": "test"}
	keyTables = map[string]string{"fixKeys": "test"}
	defer func() { dynamicPrefixes, keyTables = old, oldTables }()
	uses, dyn := keyUses(fset, f)
	uses = append(uses, tableUses(fset, f)...)
	got := map[string]int{}
	for _, u := range uses {
		got[u.key] = u.args
	}
	want := map[string]int{"a.one": 0, "a.n": 2, "e.msg": 2, "e.fix": 2, "e.msg2": 1, "e.cfg": 0, "e.cfgfix": 0, "e.tr": 1, "e.trfix": 1,
		"e.use": 1, "a.spread": -1, "e.shared": -1, "e.table": 1, "t.a": -1, "t.b": -1, "m.msg": 1, "t.say": 2}
	if len(got) != len(want) {
		t.Fatalf("uses %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
	for k, v := range want {
		if got[k] != v {
			t.Errorf("%s: args %d, want %d", k, got[k], v)
		}
	}
	if len(dyn) != 2 { // app.T(key) and other.T(key, args...) in f, where key is not a parameter
		t.Errorf("dynamic keys %v, want two", dyn)
	}
}

// hostRoot is host/, two folders above this package.
func hostRoot(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	root, err := filepath.Abs(filepath.Join("..", ".."))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(root, "go.mod")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("host/go.mod not found at %s", root)
	}
	return root
}

func maxIndex(pattern string) int {
	found, _ := verbs(pattern)
	max := 0
	for _, v := range found {
		if n, _ := strconv.Atoi(v[:strings.IndexByte(v, ':')]); n > max {
			max = n
		}
	}
	return max
}

func TestKeysExist(t *testing.T) {
	en := readCatalog(t, "en")
	used := map[string]bool{}
	// The keys of one call (NewErrorT has two: the message and its fix) share
	// its arguments: the call gives exactly as many as the most demanding key
	// uses, and no key uses more than the call gives.
	type group struct {
		args int
		max  int
	}
	groups := map[string]*group{}
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	err := filepath.WalkDir(hostRoot(t), func(path string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if d.IsDir() {
			if d.Name() == "testdata" || d.Name() == "i18n" { // the package builds its own keys
				return fs.SkipDir
			}
			return nil
		}
		if !strings.HasSuffix(path, ".go") || strings.HasSuffix(path, "_test.go") {
			return nil
		}
		f, err := parser.ParseFile(fset, path, nil, 0)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		uses, dynamic := keyUses(fset, f)
		uses = append(uses, tableUses(fset, f)...)
		for _, p := range dynamic {
			t.Errorf("%s: T, TN or NewErrorT with a key that is not a literal: declare its prefix in dynamicPrefixes", p)
		}
		for _, u := range uses {
			// A key used through TN is written key.one and key.other.
			pattern, ok := en[u.key]
			if !ok {
				if pattern, ok = en[u.key+".other"]; !ok {
					t.Errorf("%s: the key %q is not in catalog/en.toml", u.where, u.key)
					continue
				}
			}
			used[u.key] = true
			if u.args >= 0 {
				g := groups[u.where]
				if g == nil {
					g = &group{args: u.args}
					groups[u.where] = g
				}
				if n := maxIndex(pattern); n > u.args {
					t.Errorf("%s: %q takes %d argument(s) and the call gives %d", u.where, u.key, n, u.args)
				} else if n > g.max {
					g.max = n
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for where, g := range groups {
		if g.max != g.args {
			t.Errorf("%s: the call gives %d argument(s) and its keys use %d", where, g.args, g.max)
		}
	}
	for k := range en {
		base := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSuffix(k, ".one"), ".other")
		if used[k] || used[base] {
			continue
		}
		dyn := false
		for p := range dynamicPrefixes {
			dyn = dyn || strings.HasPrefix(k, p)
		}
		if !dyn {
			t.Errorf("the key %q is in the catalog and nothing uses it", k)
		}
	}
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/detect.go`:**

```go
package i18n

// Resolve is the language from what the environment says, and nothing
// outside it (no system call), in this order; the first source that has a
// value decides, and anything that is not Spanish decides English:
//
//  1. ADBUDDY_LANG
//  2. configLang, the "language" of config.toml
//  3. LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES, LANG (the first one not empty, as POSIX says)
//
// With none of them it is English. LANGUAGE is not read.
func Resolve(getenv func(string) string, configLang string) Lang {
	l, _ := resolve(getenv, configLang)
	return l
}

func resolve(getenv func(string) string, configLang string) (Lang, bool) {
	if v := getenv("ADBUDDY_LANG"); v != "" {
		return Parse(v), true
	}
	if configLang != "" {
		return Parse(configLang), true
	}
	for _, k := range []string{"LC_ALL", "LC_MESSAGES", "LANG"} {
		if v := getenv(k); v != "" {
			return Parse(v), true
		}
	}
	return EN, false
}

// Detect is Resolve, and when the environment says nothing, the language of
// the system's interface as system gives it (SystemLanguage; "" for none).
// Only the command line's entry point passes a real SystemLanguage: tests
// pass nil, so the machine they run on never changes their answers.
func Detect(getenv func(string) string, configLang string, system func() string) Lang {
	if l, ok := resolve(getenv, configLang); ok {
		return l
	}
	if system != nil {
		if v := system(); v != "" {
			return Parse(v)
		}
	}
	return EN
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/detect_test.go`:**

```go
package i18n

import "testing"

func env(m map[string]string) func(string) string { return func(k string) string { return m[k] } }

func TestParse(t *testing.T) {
	for v, want := range map[string]Lang{
		"es": ES, "ES": ES, "es_ES": ES, "es_ES.UTF-8": ES, "es-ES": ES, "es_419": ES, "es-419": ES, "es@euro": ES, "es.UTF-8": ES, " es ": ES,
		"en": EN, "en_US.UTF-8": EN, "C": EN, "POSIX": EN, "fr_FR": EN, "estonian": EN, "ess": EN, "et_EE": EN, "": EN, "e": EN,
	} {
		if got := Parse(v); got != want {
			t.Errorf("Parse(%q) = %q, want %q", v, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestResolveOrder(t *testing.T) {
	for _, c := range []struct {
		name string
		env  map[string]string
		cfg  string
		want Lang
	}{
		{"nothing", nil, "", EN},
		{"LANG es", map[string]string{"LANG": "es_ES.UTF-8"}, "", ES},
		{"LC_ALL beats LANG", map[string]string{"LC_ALL": "en_US", "LANG": "es_ES"}, "", EN},
		{"LC_ALL empty is skipped", map[string]string{"LC_ALL": "", "LC_MESSAGES": "es", "LANG": "en"}, "", ES},
		{"LC_MESSAGES beats LANG", map[string]string{"LC_MESSAGES": "es_ES", "LANG": "en_US"}, "", ES},
		{"C stops the search", map[string]string{"LC_ALL": "C", "LANG": "es_ES"}, "", EN},
		{"POSIX stops the search", map[string]string{"LANG": "POSIX"}, "", EN},
		{"LANGUAGE is not read", map[string]string{"LANGUAGE": "es"}, "", EN},
		{"config beats LANG", map[string]string{"LANG": "en_US"}, "es", ES},
		{"config en beats LANG es", map[string]string{"LANG": "es_ES"}, "en", EN},
		{"ADBUDDY_LANG beats config", map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "en"}, "es", EN},
		{"ADBUDDY_LANG beats LC_ALL", map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "es", "LC_ALL": "en_US"}, "", ES},
		{"ADBUDDY_LANG with region", map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "es_ES"}, "", ES},
		{"ADBUDDY_LANG unknown is English", map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "fr", "LANG": "es"}, "", EN},
	} {
		if got := Resolve(env(c.env), c.cfg); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: %q, want %q", c.name, got, c.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestDetectUsesTheSystemOnlyWhenTheEnvironmentIsSilent(t *testing.T) {
	system := func() string { return "es-ES" }
	if got := Detect(env(nil), "", system); got != ES {
		t.Errorf("silent environment: %q", got)
	}
	if got := Detect(env(map[string]string{"LANG": "en_US"}), "", system); got != EN {
		t.Errorf("LANG en must win over the system: %q", got)
	}
	if got := Detect(env(nil), "en", system); got != EN {
		t.Errorf("config en must win over the system: %q", got)
	}
	if got := Detect(env(map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "en"}), "", system); got != EN {
		t.Errorf("ADBUDDY_LANG must win over the system: %q", got)
	}
	if got := Detect(env(nil), "", func() string { return "" }); got != EN {
		t.Errorf("a system that says nothing: %q", got)
	}
	if got := Detect(env(nil), "", nil); got != EN {
		t.Errorf("no system: %q", got)
	}
	if got := Detect(env(nil), "", func() string { return "fr-FR" }); got != EN {
		t.Errorf("a system in French: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestSystemLanguageAnswers(t *testing.T) {
	_ = SystemLanguage() // must not panic or hang on any platform
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/detect_windows.go`:**

```go
package i18n

import "golang.org/x/sys/windows"

// SystemLanguage is the first of the user's preferred UI languages ("es-ES"),
// or "" when Windows does not say.
func SystemLanguage() string {
	langs, err := windows.GetUserPreferredUILanguages(windows.MUI_LANGUAGE_NAME)
	if err != nil || len(langs) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return langs[0]
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/i18n.go`:**

```go
// Package i18n is ADBuddy's message catalog for the host: English and Spanish,
// embedded, with no dependency beyond the TOML parser the host already uses
// and no global language. Every caller holds its own Lang, so two tests (or
// two goroutines) in different languages never step on each other.
package i18n

import (
	"embed"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"sync/atomic"

	"github.com/BurntSushi/toml"
)

// Lang is a language of the interface: "en" or "es". The zero value is
// English, as is anything that is not Spanish.
type Lang string

const (
	EN Lang = "en"
	ES Lang = "es"
)

// Norm returns EN or ES.
func (l Lang) Norm() Lang {
	if l == ES {
		return ES
	}
	return EN
}

//go:embed catalog/en.toml catalog/es.toml
var catalogFS embed.FS

// bundle holds the messages of every language.
type bundle struct{ m map[Lang]map[string]string }

func parseCatalog(data []byte) (map[string]string, error) {
	out := map[string]string{}
	if _, err := toml.Decode(string(data), &out); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return out, nil
}

func newBundle(en, es []byte) (*bundle, error) {
	e, err := parseCatalog(en)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("en.toml: %w", err)
	}
	s, err := parseCatalog(es)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("es.toml: %w", err)
	}
	return &bundle{m: map[Lang]map[string]string{EN: e, ES: s}}, nil
}

func loadEmbedded() *bundle {
	en, err := catalogFS.ReadFile("catalog/en.toml")
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	es, err := catalogFS.ReadFile("catalog/es.toml")
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	b, err := newBundle(en, es)
	if err != nil {
		panic("i18n: " + err.Error()) // a broken catalog is a programming error; TestCatalogParity catches it first
	}
	return b
}

// std is the embedded catalog. It is replaced only by WithoutKeys (tests).
var std atomic.Pointer[bundle]

func init() { std.Store(loadEmbedded()) }

// lookup finds key in lang, then in English.
func (b *bundle) lookup(l Lang, key string) (string, bool) {
	if s, ok := b.m[l.Norm()][key]; ok && s != "" {
		return s, true
	}
	if s, ok := b.m[EN][key]; ok && s != "" {
		return s, true
	}
	return "", false
}

// T returns the message key in l, formatted with args. Every verb of a
// catalog pattern has an explicit index (%[1]s, %[2]d), so that Spanish may
// reorder them and a pattern may use fewer arguments than the call gives
// (an error and its fix share the arguments of NewErrorT). A key missing in l
// is answered in English; one missing in English is answered with the key
// itself, which TestKeysExist makes impossible in a build that passes its
// tests. The patterns come from the catalog, never from args.
func (l Lang) T(key string, args ...any) string {
	s, ok := std.Load().lookup(l, key)
	if !ok {
		return key
	}
	args = l.resolve(args)
	if len(args) > 0 && !strings.Contains(strings.ReplaceAll(s, "%%", ""), "%") {
		// No verbs: fmt would append %!(EXTRA …) for the arguments the pattern does not use.
		return strings.ReplaceAll(s, "%%", "%")
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf(s, args...)
}

// Msg is a message that is not written yet: a key and its arguments. As an
// argument of T it is written in the language of that T, so a sentence can
// hold a fragment (a reason, a hint) that is a message of its own and comes
// out in the same language. In English, with fmt, it is its English.
type Msg struct {
	Key  string
	Args []any
}

// M builds a Msg.
func M(key string, args ...any) Msg { return Msg{key, args} }

// In writes m in l.
func (m Msg) In(l Lang) string { return l.T(m.Key, m.Args...) }

// String is the English of m.
func (m Msg) String() string { return m.In(EN) }

// resolve writes the Msg among args in l; args that are not a Msg stay as they are.
func (l Lang) resolve(args []any) []any {
	var out []any
	for i, a := range args {
		if m, ok := a.(Msg); ok {
			if out == nil {
				out = append([]any(nil), args...)
			}
			out[i] = m.In(l)
		}
	}
	if out == nil {
		return args
	}
	return out
}

// TN is T for a message that depends on a count: it uses key+".one" when n is
// 1 and key+".other" otherwise, and n is the first argument (%[1]d).
func (l Lang) TN(key string, n int, args ...any) string {
	suffix := ".other"
	if n == 1 {
		suffix = ".one"
	}
	return l.T(key+suffix, append([]any{n}, args...)...)
}

// Pair is a message in both languages, for the tests that compare an output
// with the catalog.
type Pair struct{ Key, EN, ES string }

// Pairs lists every message of the catalog, sorted by key.
func Pairs() []Pair {
	b := std.Load()
	out := make([]Pair, 0, len(b.m[EN]))
	for k, en := range b.m[EN] {
		out = append(out, Pair{k, en, b.m[ES][k]})
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Key < out[j].Key })
	return out
}

// Has reports whether key exists in English, the source of every catalog.
func Has(key string) bool {
	_, ok := std.Load().m[EN][key]
	return ok
}

// WithoutKeys removes keys from lang's catalog until the returned function
// restores it: a test of the fallback to English. Not for concurrent tests.
func WithoutKeys(lang Lang, keys ...string) (restore func()) {
	old := std.Load()
	nb := &bundle{m: map[Lang]map[string]string{}}
	for l, m := range old.m {
		c := make(map[string]string, len(m))
		for k, v := range m {
			c[k] = v
		}
		nb.m[l] = c
	}
	for _, k := range keys {
		delete(nb.m[lang.Norm()], k)
	}
	std.Store(nb)
	return func() { std.Store(old) }
}

// Parse reads a language name as written in ADBUDDY_LANG, config.toml or the
// POSIX locale variables ("es", "es_ES.UTF-8", "es-419", "es@euro"): Spanish
// when it starts with "es" followed by nothing or one of _ - . @; English for
// anything else, "C" and "POSIX" included.
func Parse(v string) Lang {
	v = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(v))
	if rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(v, "es"); ok {
		if rest == "" || strings.ContainsRune("_-.@", rune(rest[0])) {
			return ES
		}
	}
	return EN
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/i18n_test.go`:**

```go
package i18n

import "testing"

func testBundle(t *testing.T, en, es string) func() {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := newBundle([]byte(en), []byte(es))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	old := std.Load()
	std.Store(b)
	return func() { std.Store(old) }
}

func TestTAndFallback(t *testing.T) {
	defer testBundle(t, `
"a.hello" = "Hello %[1]s"
"a.only_en" = "Only English"
"a.order" = "%[2]s then %[1]s"
`, `
"a.hello" = "Hola %[1]s"
"a.order" = "%[2]s y luego %[1]s"
`)()
	cases := []struct {
		lang Lang
		key  string
		args []any
		want string
	}{
		{ES, "a.hello", []any{"Ana"}, "Hola Ana"},
		{EN, "a.hello", []any{"Ana"}, "Hello Ana"},
		{"", "a.hello", []any{"Ana"}, "Hello Ana"},   // the zero Lang is English
		{"fr", "a.hello", []any{"Ana"}, "Hello Ana"}, // anything else too
		{ES, "a.only_en", nil, "Only English"},       // missing in es: English, never empty
		{ES, "a.order", []any{"uno", "dos"}, "dos y luego uno"},
		{ES, "a.nowhere", nil, "a.nowhere"}, // missing everywhere: the key
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := c.lang.T(c.key, c.args...); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%q.T(%s) = %q, want %q", c.lang, c.key, got, c.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestTNeverUsesTheArgumentAsPattern(t *testing.T) {
	defer testBundle(t, `"a.say" = "said: %[1]s"`, `"a.say" = "dijo: %[1]s"`)()
	if got := ES.T("a.say", "%d %s %[3]q"); got != "dijo: %d %s %[3]q" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", got)
	}
}

func TestTN(t *testing.T) {
	defer testBundle(t, `
"u.done.one" = "Undid %[1]d change."
"u.done.other" = "Undid %[1]d changes."
"u.of.one" = "%[1]d change in %[2]s"
"u.of.other" = "%[1]d changes in %[2]s"
`, `
"u.done.one" = "Se deshizo %[1]d cambio."
"u.done.other" = "Se deshicieron %[1]d cambios."
"u.of.one" = "%[1]d cambio en %[2]s"
"u.of.other" = "%[1]d cambios en %[2]s"
`)()
	for _, c := range []struct {
		lang Lang
		n    int
		want string
	}{{EN, 1, "Undid 1 change."}, {EN, 0, "Undid 0 changes."}, {EN, 2, "Undid 2 changes."}, {ES, 1, "Se deshizo 1 cambio."}, {ES, 3, "Se deshicieron 3 cambios."}} {
		if got := c.lang.TN("u.done", c.n); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%q.TN(%d) = %q, want %q", c.lang, c.n, got, c.want)
		}
	}
	if got := ES.TN("u.of", 2, "el diario"); got != "2 cambios en el diario" {
		t.Errorf("TN with an argument: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestWithoutKeysFallsBackToEnglishAndRestores(t *testing.T) {
	defer testBundle(t, `"k.x" = "English"`, `"k.x" = "Español"`)()
	restore := WithoutKeys(ES, "k.x")
	if got := ES.T("k.x"); got != "English" {
		t.Errorf("without the key: %q", got)
	}
	if got := EN.T("k.x"); got != "English" {
		t.Errorf("English changed: %q", got)
	}
	restore()
	if got := ES.T("k.x"); got != "Español" {
		t.Errorf("after restore: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestEmbeddedCatalogsParse(t *testing.T) {
	if std.Load() == nil || len(std.Load().m) != 2 {
		t.Fatal("the embedded catalogs did not load")
	}
	if Has("no.such.key") {
		t.Fatal("Has invented a key")
	}
}

func TestAPatternWithoutVerbsIgnoresTheArguments(t *testing.T) {
	defer testBundle(t, `
"a.fix" = "Run `+"`adbuddy setup`"+`."
"a.pct" = "100%% done"
"a.two" = "only the second: %[2]s"
`, `
"a.fix" = "Ejecuta `+"`adbuddy setup`"+`."
"a.pct" = "100%% hecho"
"a.two" = "solo el segundo: %[2]s"
`)()
	if got := ES.T("a.fix", "x", 2); got != "Ejecuta `adbuddy setup`." {
		t.Errorf("an error and its fix share the arguments: %q", got)
	}
	if got := ES.T("a.pct"); got != "100% hecho" {
		t.Errorf("%%%% without arguments: %q", got)
	}
	if got := ES.T("a.pct", 1); got != "100% hecho" {
		t.Errorf("%%%% with arguments: %q", got)
	}
	if got := ES.T("a.two", "uno", "dos"); got != "solo el segundo: dos" {
		t.Errorf("an unused argument: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestAMsgAsAnArgumentIsWrittenInTheLanguageOfTheSentence(t *testing.T) {
	defer testBundle(t, `
"s.warn" = "Warning: the phone at %[1]s %[2]s."
"s.why" = "is not the one you set up"
"s.nested" = "cannot be checked: %[1]s"
"s.leaf" = "no phone is set up in %[1]s"
`, `
"s.warn" = "Aviso: el móvil en %[1]s %[2]s."
"s.why" = "no es el que configuraste"
"s.nested" = "no se puede comprobar: %[1]s"
"s.leaf" = "no hay ningún móvil configurado en %[1]s"
`)()
	if got := ES.T("s.warn", "192.0.2.2", M("s.why")); got != "Aviso: el móvil en 192.0.2.2 no es el que configuraste." {
		t.Errorf("es: %q", got)
	}
	if got := EN.T("s.warn", "192.0.2.2", M("s.why")); got != "Warning: the phone at 192.0.2.2 is not the one you set up." {
		t.Errorf("en: %q", got)
	}
	if got := ES.T("s.nested", M("s.nested", M("s.leaf", "config.toml"))); got != "no se puede comprobar: no se puede comprobar: no hay ningún móvil configurado en config.toml" {
		t.Errorf("a Msg inside a Msg: %q", got)
	}
	if got := M("s.why").String(); got != "is not the one you set up" {
		t.Errorf("String is the English: %q", got)
	}
	args := []any{"a", M("s.why")}
	_ = ES.T("s.warn", args...)
	if _, still := args[1].(Msg); !still {
		t.Errorf("T must not change the caller's arguments")
	}
}
```

```go
// Package i18ntest has the helpers of the tests that run in Spanish.
package i18ntest

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/i18n"
)

// Lang makes ADBUDDY_LANG name lang for the test (the highest source of
// i18n.Resolve) and returns it.
func Lang(t testing.TB, lang i18n.Lang) i18n.Lang {
	t.Helper()
	t.Setenv("ADBUDDY_LANG", string(lang))
	return lang
}

// Without removes keys from lang's catalog for the test, to see what the
// fallback to English shows. The test must not run in parallel.
func Without(t testing.TB, lang i18n.Lang, keys ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	t.Cleanup(i18n.WithoutKeys(lang, keys...))
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/i18ntest/noenglish.go`:**

```go
package i18ntest

import (
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/i18n"
)

var verbRE = regexp.MustCompile(`%\[\d+\][sdvqxXtf]`)
var keyLine = regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^\s*[a-z][a-z0-9]*(\.[a-z0-9_]+)+\s*$`)

// NoEnglish fails the test when out, written in Spanish, still has a message
// of the catalog in English (a text that was not looked up in the language),
// or shows the name of a key. except lists the keys whose English may appear
// on purpose. See Problems for what is looked for.
func NoEnglish(t testing.TB, out string, except ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, p := range Problems(out, i18n.Pairs(), except...) {
		t.Error(p)
	}
}

// Problems is NoEnglish without the test: the English messages of pairs found
// in out (a message with fewer than 12 characters of fixed text is not looked
// for: it would match by chance), and the names of keys shown as text.
func Problems(out string, pairs []i18n.Pair, except ...string) []string {
	skip := map[string]bool{}
	for _, k := range except {
		skip[k] = true
	}
	var found []string
	for _, p := range pairs {
		if p.EN == p.ES || skip[p.Key] {
			continue
		}
		if len(strings.TrimSpace(verbRE.ReplaceAllString(p.EN, ""))) < 12 {
			continue
		}
		parts := verbRE.Split(p.EN, -1)
		for i, s := range parts {
			parts[i] = regexp.QuoteMeta(s)
		}
		if regexp.MustCompile(strings.Join(parts, `[^\n]*?`)).MatchString(out) {
			found = append(found, "the output has the English of "+p.Key+": "+p.EN)
		}
	}
	if k := keyLine.FindString(out); k != "" {
		found = append(found, "the output shows the name of a catalog key: "+strings.TrimSpace(k))
	}
	return found
}

var phraseRE = regexp.MustCompile(`\p{L}{2,}\s+\p{L}{2,}`)

// Untranslated are the lines that a command writes the same in English and in
// Spanish and that have a phrase in them (two words): text that was never
// moved to the catalog and that NoEnglish cannot see, because it is not in it.
// allow lists substrings of lines that may be the same on purpose (an order, a
// path, a serial, a name). Run the same scenario twice, once in each language.
func Untranslated(en, es string, allow ...string) []string {
	inEN := map[string]bool{}
	for _, l := range strings.Split(en, "\n") {
		inEN[strings.TrimSpace(l)] = true
	}
	var found []string
lines:
	for _, l := range strings.Split(es, "\n") {
		l = strings.TrimSpace(l)
		if !inEN[l] || !phraseRE.MatchString(l) {
			continue
		}
		for _, a := range allow {
			if strings.Contains(l, a) {
				continue lines
			}
		}
		found = append(found, l)
	}
	return found
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/i18n/i18ntest/noenglish_test.go`:**

```go
package i18ntest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/i18n"
)

func TestProblems(t *testing.T) {
	pairs := []i18n.Pair{
		{Key: "a.hello", EN: "Hello %[1]s, welcome back", ES: "Hola %[1]s, bienvenido"},
		{Key: "a.short", EN: "Done", ES: "Hecho"},
		{Key: "a.same", EN: "Same text for everyone", ES: "Same text for everyone"},
		{Key: "a.cmd", EN: "Run `adbuddy setup` now", ES: "Ejecuta `adbuddy setup` ahora"},
	}
	has := func(out string, except []string, want ...string) {
		t.Helper()
		got := strings.Join(Problems(out, pairs, except...), "\n")
		for _, w := range want {
			if !strings.Contains(got, w) {
				t.Errorf("%q: %q lacks %q", out, got, w)
			}
		}
		if len(want) == 0 && got != "" {
			t.Errorf("%q: unexpected %q", out, got)
		}
	}
	has("Hola Ana, bienvenido\nHecho", nil)
	has("line\nHello Ana, welcome back\n", nil, "a.hello")
	has("Hello Ana, welcome back", []string{"a.hello"})
	has("Done", nil)                   // too short to look for
	has("Same text for everyone", nil) // the same in both languages
	has("Run `adbuddy setup` now", nil, "a.cmd")
	has("a.hello", nil, "the name of a catalog key")
	has("doctor.check.adb.missing\n", nil, "the name of a catalog key")
	has("see adbuddy.exe and v1.2.3 for details", nil) // not keys
}

func TestUntranslated(t *testing.T) {
	en := "Getting adb…\nRun: adbuddy setup\nPhone: Pixel 9, Android 17.\nNote: not translated yet\n"
	es := "Obteniendo adb…\nRun: adbuddy setup\nMóvil: Pixel 9, Android 17.\nNote: not translated yet\n"
	got := Untranslated(en, es)
	if len(got) != 2 || got[0] != "Run: adbuddy setup" || got[1] != "Note: not translated yet" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", got)
	}
	if got := Untranslated(en, es, "Run: adbuddy"); len(got) != 1 || got[0] != "Note: not translated yet" {
		t.Fatalf("with an allowed line: %q", got)
	}
}
```

```go
// Lang is the language for rule descriptions: the configured one, else "es"
// when LC_ALL/LC_MESSAGES/LANG start with "es", else "en".
func (c Config) Lang(getenv func(string) string) string {
	if c.Language != "" {
		return c.Language
	}
	for _, k := range []string{"LC_ALL", "LC_MESSAGES", "LANG"} {
		if v := getenv(k); v != "" {
			if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(v), "es") {
				return "es"
			}
			return "en"
		}
	}
	return "en"
}
```

por

```go
// Lang is the language of the host's texts as the environment and this
// config give it (i18n.Resolve): ADBUDDY_LANG, then language, then
// LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES and LANG, else "en". It does not ask the system; the
// command line's entry point does (i18n.Detect), so that serve and its
// daemon never disagree with the help.
func (c Config) Lang(getenv func(string) string) string {
	return string(i18n.Resolve(getenv, c.Language))
}

// ReadLanguage is the "language" of the config.toml at path, "" when the file
// is missing, unreadable or does not say. It ignores every other key and
// every error: a broken configuration must not keep --help or an error
// message from appearing in the right language.
func ReadLanguage(path string) string {
	var c struct {
		Language string `toml:"language"`
	}
	if _, err := toml.DecodeFile(path, &c); err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	switch c.Language {
	case "en", "es":
		return c.Language
	}
	return ""
}
```

```go
func TestLangFollowsADBUDDYLangFirst(t *testing.T) {
	env := func(m map[string]string) func(string) string { return func(k string) string { return m[k] } }
	c := Default()
	c.Language = "es"
	if got := c.Lang(env(map[string]string{"ADBUDDY_LANG": "en", "LANG": "es_ES"})); got != "en" {
		t.Fatalf("ADBUDDY_LANG must win over config.language: %s", got)
	}
	if got := Default().Lang(env(map[string]string{"LC_ALL": "C", "LANG": "es_ES"})); got != "en" {
		t.Fatalf("LC_ALL=C decides: %s", got)
	}
}

func TestReadLanguage(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	write := func(name, content string) string {
		p := filepath.Join(dir, name)
		if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(content), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return p
	}
	for _, c := range []struct{ name, content, want string }{
		{"es", "language = \"es\"\n", "es"},
		{"en", "language = \"en\"\n", "en"},
		{"other keys do not matter", "typo_key = 1\nserial = \"abc\"\nlanguage = \"es\"\n", "es"},
		{"unknown language", "language = \"fr\"\n", ""},
		{"no language", "serial = \"abc\"\n", ""},
		{"broken toml", "language = \n[[", ""},
	} {
		if got := ReadLanguage(write(c.name+".toml", c.content)); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: %q, want %q", c.name, got, c.want)
		}
	}
	if got := ReadLanguage(filepath.Join(dir, "missing.toml")); got != "" {
		t.Errorf("a missing file: %q", got)
	}
}
```

```go
func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
	// The language of the machine never changes what the tests read: English
	// (ADBUDDY_LANG beats config.language, LANG and the language of Windows).
	os.Setenv("ADBUDDY_LANG", "en")
```


**Editar `.gitattributes`** (cada bloque: lo que hay, y por lo que se cambia; el texto de «lo que hay» aparece una sola vez en el fichero):

1. Sustituye

```
host/**/testdata/** text eol=lf
```

por

```
host/**/testdata/** text eol=lf
host/**/*.toml text eol=lf
```

2. Sustituye

```
android/**/*.properties text eol=lf
```

por

```
android/**/*.properties text eol=lf
android/**/*.txt text eol=lf
```

(El segundo bloque es para `android/app/src/test/resources/i18n-exceptions.txt` de A1. **`.gitattributes` solo lo edita H0**: H0 y A1 pueden ir a la vez y los dos tocaban el mismo fichero; A1 ya no lo toca. Si A1 termina antes de que H0 haga su commit, no pasa nada: con `core.autocrlf=true` el fichero se normaliza al añadirlo y V comprueba con `git ls-files --eol android/app/src/test/resources/i18n-exceptions.txt` que queda `i/lf`.)


El de `e2e_test.go` va en un commit propio (`test(e2e): TestMain fija ADBUDDY_LANG=en`), porque es de M3/M6a:

**Editar `host/cmd/adbuddy/e2e_test.go`** (cada bloque: lo que hay, y por lo que se cambia; el texto de «lo que hay» aparece una sola vez en el fichero):

1. Sustituye

```go
func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
```

por

```go
func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
	// The binaries the tests start inherit it: English whatever the machine.
	os.Setenv("ADBUDDY_LANG", "en")
```

```bash
cd host
gofmt -l .                                   # vacío
go vet ./...
GOOS=linux go vet ./... && GOOS=darwin go vet ./...
go test -count=1 -timeout 120s ./internal/i18n/... ./internal/config/
```

Esperado: todo verde, con el catálogo vacío (los controles están activos pero no tienen nada que comparar; sus analizadores se prueban con fuentes sintéticas dentro de los propios tests).

- [ ] **Paso 5: Regenerar el contador y compararlo.** `testdata/textlint.txt` es la medida hecha sobre el árbol de `m4`: 325 literales (cobra 36, flag 30, error 82, print 138, check 39). En el árbol de `m6c` puede diferir por M6b (`envcmd.go`):

```bash

git diff --stat internal/cli/testdata/textlint.txt
```
Si cambia, el diff solo debe tocar `envcmd.go` (M6b); anota los números nuevos en el ledger y sigue con **el fichero regenerado**. El test `TestTextLintSeesWhatItShould` fija lo que cada regla detecta.

- [ ] **Paso 6: Suite completa** (`go test -count=1 -timeout 580s ./...`, en segundo plano si hace falta). Esperado: verde. Un `TestMain` que ponga `ADBUDDY_LANG=en` solo hace falta en `cli` y `cmd/adbuddy` (los demás paquetes reciben el idioma como argumento; la spec §9.1 listaba también `daemon`, `confirm`, `session` y `claudecode`: no leen el entorno, un `TestMain` no haría nada).

```bash
git add host/internal/i18n host/internal/config host/internal/cli/lang.go host/internal/cli/lang_test.go \
  host/internal/cli/console_windows.go host/internal/cli/console_other.go host/internal/cli/textlint_test.go \
  host/internal/cli/testdata/textlint.txt host/internal/cli/root.go host/internal/cli/main_test.go .gitattributes
git commit -m "feat(host): paquete i18n, detección de idioma y controles de catálogo" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
git add host/cmd/adbuddy/e2e_test.go
git commit -m "test(e2e): TestMain fija ADBUDDY_LANG=en" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

**Criterios de aceptación de H0**
1. `go test` de `i18n`, `i18ntest`, `config`, `cli` y `cmd/adbuddy` verdes; los tests existentes sin tocar sus aserciones.
2. `ADBUDDY_LANG=en` está en `TestMain` de `cli` y de `cmd/adbuddy`; `TestTestMainPinsEnglish` lo comprueba.
3. Orden de detección probado con tabla: `ADBUDDY_LANG` → `config.language` → `LC_ALL`/`LC_MESSAGES`/`LANG` (el primero **no vacío** decide; `C`/`POSIX`/otro → `en` y no se sigue) → idioma de Windows (solo en `mainContext`, nunca en los tests) → `en`; `LANGUAGE` no se lee; `es`, `es_ES.UTF-8`, `es-419`, `es@euro` → `es`; `estonian` → `en`.
4. `--json` fuerza inglés también para la ayuda (`wantsJSON` antes de `NewRoot`) y `app.Getenv("ADBUDDY_LANG")` devuelve el idioma ya decidido (así `Config.Lang(app.Getenv)` en `serve` no se desincroniza de la ayuda, Windows incluido).
5. Un `config.toml` roto no impide leer `language` (`ReadLanguage` ignora el resto y los errores).
6. `go vet` limpio en windows, linux y darwin; `gofmt -l` vacío.
