## Tarea A1: Controles de la app (paridad de recursos, texto suelto, lint)

**Árbol:** `android/` · **Revisor:** `adbuddy-reviewer` · **Commit:** `test(android): controles de i18n (paridad de recursos, texto suelto y lint)`

**Qué hace y qué no:** añade dos tests JVM y un bloque de lint, sin cambiar el comportamiento de la app salvo un arreglo que el propio control destapó: `consent_intro` lleva `"%1$s"` con comillas sin escapar en los dos idiomas y AAPT2 **las quita** (la persona nunca vio las comillas). Las dos únicas líneas de texto suelto que hoy detecta el escáner (`ActivityScreen`: `"· adb"` y `"↩ adb"`) entran en `i18n-exceptions.txt` con su motivo, y A2 las arregla y las quita.

**Ficheros:**
- Crear: `android/app/src/test/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/i18n/StringsParityTest.kt`, `…/i18n/NoHardcodedTextTest.kt`, `android/app/src/test/resources/i18n-exceptions.txt`.
- Modificar: `android/app/build.gradle.kts` (bloque `lint` y propiedades de sistema con las rutas de recursos y fuentes), `android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml` y `values-es/strings.xml` (solo `consent_intro`). **No toca `.gitattributes`** (la regla `android/**/*.txt` la añade H0).

**Interfaces que produce:** `StringsCheck.parse/problems/markers/escapingProblem` y `StringsCheck.sameInBoth` (lista corta de textos iguales en los dos idiomas, cada uno con su motivo; empieza con `perm_deny`); `Lexer.lex` y `HardcodedText.scan(file, src, exceptions)`; propiedades `adbuddy.resDir` y `adbuddy.kotlinDir`.

**Cómo funciona `NoHardcodedTextTest`** (no es un parser de Kotlin: es un lexer pequeño que quita comentarios y sustituye cada literal por `"S"`, más reglas): (1) un literal con letras como argumento de `Text(`, `setContentTitle(`, `setContentText(`, `setSubText(`, `setTicker(`, `Toast.makeText(`, `NotificationChannel(`, `setName(`, `setLabel(`, `addAction(`, `Action.Builder(`, `AlertDialog(`, `Snackbar.make(`, o dado a `text =`, `contentDescription =`, `label =`, `title =`, `placeholder =`, `subtitle =`, `hint =`, en cualquier fichero; (2) un literal con frase (dos palabras) en `ui/`, `notify/`, `confirm/`, `control/`, `files/`, `AdbuddyApp.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `ChatBubbleActivity.kt`, `bridge/BridgeService.kt` y `a11y/Overlay.kt`. Se salta lo que está dentro de una función con una anotación `@…Preview…` (el proyecto usa `@AdbuddyPreviews`), los comentarios y las líneas con `Log.`, `throw `, `require(`, `check(`, `error(`, `assert(`. `a11y/UiExecutor.kt` (mensajes al agente) y `term/QuickKeys.kt` (nombres de tecla) no están en la lista de la regla 2 a propósito. Cada línea de `i18n-exceptions.txt` que ya no case con nada hace fallar el test (`everyExceptionStillMatchesSomething`).

- [ ] **Paso 1: Línea base.** Desde `android/`: `gradle :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:lintDebug`. Esperado: verde (434 tests, sin errores de lint). Si no, detente: el árbol no es el medido.

- [ ] **Paso 2: Crear los ficheros** (los tests ya son el rojo: no compilan hasta el paso 3).

**Crear `android/app/src/test/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/i18n/NoHardcodedTextTest.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.i18n

import java.io.File
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/** A string literal of a Kotlin source: its text with the `${…}` and `$name` templates taken out, and its line. */
class Literal(val text: String, val line: Int)

/** The source with comments gone and every string and character literal replaced by "S" (line breaks kept). */
class Lexed(val skeleton: String, val literals: List<Literal>)

object Lexer {
    fun lex(src: String): Lexed {
        val sk = StringBuilder()
        val lits = mutableListOf<Literal>()
        var line = 1
        var i = 0
        fun newline() { line++ }

        // Reads a string body from i (after the opening quote) to its closing quote; returns the text without templates.
        fun readString(raw: Boolean): String {
            val text = StringBuilder()
            while (i < src.length) {
                val c = src[i]
                when {
                    !raw && c == '\\' -> { text.append(c); if (i + 1 < src.length) text.append(src[i + 1]); i += 2 }
                    !raw && c == '"' -> { i++; return text.toString() }
                    raw && src.startsWith("\"\"\"", i) -> {
                        i += 3
                        while (i < src.length && src[i] == '"') i++ // """" ends at the last quote
                        return text.toString()
                    }
                    c == '$' && i + 1 < src.length && src[i + 1] == '{' -> {
                        i += 2
                        var depth = 1
                        while (i < src.length && depth > 0) {
                            when (src[i]) {
                                '{' -> depth++
                                '}' -> depth--
                                '"' -> { i++; readString(false); continue } // a string inside the template
                                '\n' -> newline()
                            }
                            i++
                        }
                    }
                    c == '$' && i + 1 < src.length && (src[i + 1].isLetter() || src[i + 1] == '_') -> {
                        i++
                        while (i < src.length && (src[i].isLetterOrDigit() || src[i] == '_')) i++
                    }
                    else -> { if (c == '\n') newline(); text.append(c); i++ }
                }
            }
            return text.toString()
        }

        while (i < src.length) {
            val c = src[i]
            when {
                src.startsWith("//", i) -> while (i < src.length && src[i] != '\n') i++
                src.startsWith("/*", i) -> {
                    var depth = 0
                    while (i < src.length) {
                        if (src.startsWith("/*", i)) { depth++; i += 2 }
                        else if (src.startsWith("*/", i)) { depth--; i += 2; if (depth == 0) break }
                        else { if (src[i] == '\n') { newline(); sk.append('\n') }; i++ }
                    }
                }
                src.startsWith("\"\"\"", i) -> {
                    val start = line
                    i += 3
                    val text = readString(true)
                    lits += Literal(text, start)
                    sk.append("\"S\"")
                    repeat(line - start) { sk.append('\n') }
                }
                c == '"' -> {
                    val start = line
                    i++
                    val text = readString(false)
                    lits += Literal(text, start)
                    sk.append("\"S\"")
                    repeat(line - start) { sk.append('\n') }
                }
                c == '\'' -> { // a character literal: '{', '\'', 'A'
                    i++
                    while (i < src.length && src[i] != '\'') { if (src[i] == '\\') i++; i++ }
                    i++
                    sk.append("'c'")
                }
                else -> { if (c == '\n') newline(); sk.append(c); i++ }
            }
        }
        return Lexed(sk.toString(), lits)
    }
}

/** Where a literal sits in the skeleton: the index of its "S" and its place in `literals`. */
private class Spot(val skIndex: Int, val lit: Literal)

class Finding(val file: String, val line: Int, val text: String, val why: String) {
    override fun toString() = "$file:$line: \"$text\" ($why)"
}

object HardcodedText {
    /** Calls whose literal arguments are text a person sees. */
    private val sinkCalls = listOf(
        "Text", "setContentTitle", "setContentText", "setSubText", "setTicker", "Toast.makeText", "NotificationChannel",
        "setName", "setLabel", "addAction", "Action.Builder", "AlertDialog", "Snackbar.make",
    )

    /** Named arguments (or assignments) whose literal value is text a person sees. */
    private val sinkNames = listOf("text", "contentDescription", "label", "title", "placeholder", "subtitle", "hint")

    /** Where phrases (two words or more) are looked for too: the files that build what a person sees. */
    val phrasePaths = listOf("ui/", "notify/", "confirm/", "control/", "files/", "AdbuddyApp.kt", "MainActivity.kt", "ChatBubbleActivity.kt", "bridge/BridgeService.kt", "a11y/Overlay.kt")

    private val phrase = Regex("\\p{L}{2,}\\s+\\p{L}{2,}")
    private val hasLetter = Regex("\\p{L}")
    private val previewAnnotation = Regex("@\\w*Preview\\w*")
    private val skipTokens = listOf("Log.", "throw ", "require(", "check(", "error(", "assert(")

    /** Matches the paren that closes the one at [open] in the skeleton, or -1. */
    private fun close(sk: String, open: Int): Int {
        var depth = 0
        for (j in open until sk.length) {
            when (sk[j]) {
                '(', '{', '[' -> depth++
                ')', '}', ']' -> { depth--; if (depth == 0) return j }
            }
        }
        return -1
    }

    /** The skeleton indexes of "S" at depth 0 of sk[from, to). */
    private fun topLevelLiterals(sk: String, from: Int, to: Int): List<Int> {
        val out = mutableListOf<Int>()
        var depth = 0
        var j = from
        while (j < to) {
            when (sk[j]) {
                '(', '{', '[' -> depth++
                ')', '}', ']' -> depth--
                '"' -> if (depth == 0 && sk.startsWith("\"S\"", j)) { out += j; j += 2 }
            }
            j++
        }
        return out
    }

    /** The end of the expression a named argument or assignment starts at [from]: a `,`, a `)` that closes the call, or the line's end, at depth 0. */
    private fun expressionEnd(sk: String, from: Int): Int {
        var depth = 0
        var j = from
        while (j < sk.length) {
            when (sk[j]) {
                '(', '{', '[' -> depth++
                ')', '}', ']' -> { if (depth == 0) return j; depth-- }
                ',' -> if (depth == 0) return j
                '\n' -> if (depth == 0) {
                    // a line that ends with an operator goes on in the next one
                    val before = sk.substring(from, j).trimEnd()
                    if (!(before.endsWith("+") || before.endsWith("?:") || before.endsWith("else") || before.endsWith("->"))) return j
                }
            }
            j++
        }
        return sk.length
    }

    /** The regions of the skeleton that are the body of a function with @Preview: no one reads them as text. */
    private fun previewRegions(sk: String): List<IntRange> {
        val out = mutableListOf<IntRange>()
        var from = 0
        while (true) {
            val at = previewAnnotation.find(sk, from)?.range?.first ?: -1
            if (at < 0) break
            val fn = sk.indexOf("fun ", at)
            if (fn < 0) break
            val open = sk.indexOf('{', fn)
            val eq = sk.indexOf('=', fn)
            val end = if (open >= 0 && (eq < 0 || open < eq)) close(sk, open) else sk.indexOf('\n', if (eq >= 0) eq else fn)
            if (end < 0) break
            out += at..end
            from = end
        }
        return out
    }

    /** The text of a literal as a person would see it: the letters it has. */
    private fun visible(l: Literal) = hasLetter.containsMatchIn(l.text)

    fun scan(file: String, src: String, exceptions: Set<String>): List<Finding> {
        val lex = Lexer.lex(src)
        val sk = lex.skeleton
        val spots = mutableListOf<Spot>()
        var n = 0
        var k = sk.indexOf("\"S\"")
        while (k >= 0 && n < lex.literals.size) {
            spots += Spot(k, lex.literals[n++])
            k = sk.indexOf("\"S\"", k + 3)
        }
        val byIndex = spots.associateBy { it.skIndex }
        val previews = previewRegions(sk)
        val lines = src.lines()
        fun skipped(l: Literal, at: Int): Boolean {
            if (previews.any { at in it }) return true
            if ("$file|${l.text.trim()}" in exceptions || "$file|*" in exceptions) return true
            // The literal's line and the two before it (a statement that goes on over several lines).
            val window = (maxOf(0, l.line - 3)..minOf(lines.size - 1, l.line - 1)).joinToString(" ") { lines[it] }
            return skipTokens.any { window.contains(it) }
        }

        val found = LinkedHashMap<Int, Finding>()
        fun flag(index: Int, why: String) {
            val spot = byIndex[index] ?: return
            if (!visible(spot.lit) || skipped(spot.lit, index)) return
            found.putIfAbsent(index, Finding(file, spot.lit.line, spot.lit.text, why))
        }

        // 1. A literal as an argument of a call that shows text, in any file.
        for (name in sinkCalls) {
            Regex("(?<!\\w)" + Regex.escape(name) + "\\s*\\(").findAll(sk).forEach { m ->
                val open = m.range.last
                val end = close(sk, open)
                if (end > 0) topLevelLiterals(sk, open + 1, end).forEach { flag(it, "a literal in $name(…)") }
            }
        }
        // 2. A literal given to a named argument that is text: text = "…", contentDescription = "…".
        for (name in sinkNames) {
            Regex("(?<!\\w)" + name + "\\s*=(?!=)\\s*").findAll(sk).forEach { m ->
                val from = m.range.last + 1
                topLevelLiterals(sk, from, expressionEnd(sk, from)).forEach { flag(it, "a literal given to $name =") }
            }
        }
        // 3. A phrase (two words or more) in the files that build what a person sees.
        if (phrasePaths.any { file == it || file.startsWith(it) }) {
            for (spot in spots) if (phrase.containsMatchIn(spot.lit.text)) flag(spot.skIndex, "a phrase in a file that builds what a person sees")
        }
        return found.values.toList()
    }
}

class NoHardcodedTextTest {
    private val root = File(System.getProperty("adbuddy.kotlinDir"))

    /** `file|text` per line: a literal that is not for a person, with why. `file|*` exempts the whole file. */
    private fun exceptions(): Set<String> {
        val text = javaClass.getResourceAsStream("/i18n-exceptions.txt")!!.bufferedReader().readText()
        return text.lines().map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() && !it.startsWith("#") }.map { line ->
            val parts = line.split(" | ")
            assertTrue("i18n-exceptions.txt: \"$line\" must be `file | text | why`", parts.size >= 3 && parts[2].isNotBlank())
            parts[0].trim() + "|" + parts[1].trim()
        }.toSet()
    }

    @Test fun noTextForAPersonIsWrittenInTheCode() {
        val ex = exceptions()
        val findings = root.walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile && it.extension == "kt" }.sortedBy { it.path }.flatMap { f ->
            HardcodedText.scan(f.relativeTo(File(root, "tech/hybriq/adbuddy")).invariantSeparatorsPath, f.readText(), ex)
        }.toList()
        assertTrue("Text a person sees must come from strings.xml (values and values-es). Found:\n" + findings.joinToString("\n") +
            "\nIf it is not for a person (a message to the agent, a key name), add it to src/test/resources/i18n-exceptions.txt with its reason.", findings.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun everyExceptionStillMatchesSomething() {
        val sources = root.walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile && it.extension == "kt" }
            .associate { it.relativeTo(File(root, "tech/hybriq/adbuddy")).invariantSeparatorsPath to it.readText() }
        val stale = exceptions().filter { e ->
            val (file, text) = e.split("|", limit = 2)
            val src = sources[file] ?: return@filter true
            text != "*" && !src.contains(text)
        }
        assertTrue("i18n-exceptions.txt lists literals that are no longer in the code: $stale", stale.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun theScannerSeesWhatItShould() {
        fun scan(src: String, file: String = "ui/X.kt") = HardcodedText.scan(file, src, emptySet()).map { it.text }
        assertEquals(listOf("Hello"), scan("""fun a() { Text("Hello") }"""))
        assertEquals(listOf("Hello"), scan("""fun a() { Text(text = "Hello", modifier = m) }"""))
        assertEquals(listOf("Hello"), scan("""fun a() { Icon(x, contentDescription = "Hello") }"""))
        assertEquals(listOf("Sending"), scan("""fun a() { Text(if (b) "Sending" else stringResource(R.string.x)) }"""))
        assertEquals(listOf("Hi"), scan("""fun a() = NotificationCompat.Builder(c, id).setContentTitle("Hi")"""))
        assertEquals(listOf("Hi"), scan("""fun a() { Toast.makeText(c, "Hi", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show() }""", "bridge/X.kt"))
        assertEquals(listOf("Conversation with the agent"), scan("""fun a() { NotificationChannel("c", "Conversation with the agent", 1) }""", "AdbuddyApp.kt").drop(1))
        assertEquals(listOf("Read the battery level"), scan("""val a = JournalEntry(description = "Read the battery level")"""))
        // What is not text for a person.
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { Text(stringResource(R.string.hello)) }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { Text("") ; Text("·"); Text("→ ${'$'}x") }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { Text(foo("Hello")) }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { // Text("Hello") in a comment
            /* Text("Hello") */ }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""val a = "no phrase here_but_one_word""", "bridge/Y.kt"))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { Log.w(TAG, "could not go to the foreground") }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { throw IllegalStateException("not ready yet") }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""fun a() { require(x) { "must not be empty" } }"""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""@Preview @Composable private fun P() { Text("Hello there") ; Foo(text = "Hi") }
            @Composable fun Q() { Text(stringResource(R.string.x)) }"""))
        // A phrase in a file that builds what a person sees; the same elsewhere is not looked at.
        assertEquals(listOf("two words"), scan("""val s = "two words" """, "notify/Z.kt"))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), scan("""val s = "two words" """, "bridge/Z.kt"))
        // Templates are not words, strings inside templates do not break the reading, and raw strings work.
        assertEquals(listOf("Hello  there"), scan("""val s = "Hello ${'$'}{name.ifEmpty { "x y" }} there" """, "notify/Z.kt"))
        assertEquals(listOf("one two"), scan("val s = \"\"\"one two\"\"\"", "ui/Z.kt"))
        // Exceptions by file and text, or by file.
        val e = HardcodedText.scan("a11y/Overlay.kt", """val s = "to the agent" """, setOf("a11y/Overlay.kt|to the agent"))
        assertTrue(e.isEmpty())
        assertTrue(HardcodedText.scan("a11y/Overlay.kt", """val s = "to the agent" """, setOf("a11y/Overlay.kt|*")).isEmpty())
    }
}
```

**Crear `android/app/src/test/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/i18n/StringsParityTest.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.i18n

import java.io.File
import java.io.StringReader
import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.w3c.dom.Element
import org.xml.sax.InputSource

/** One `<string>` or `<plurals>` of a strings.xml: the texts by quantity ("" for a plain string). */
class Res(val name: String, val translatable: Boolean, val plural: Boolean, val texts: Map<String, String>)

object StringsCheck {
    fun parse(xml: String): Map<String, Res> {
        val doc = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance().newDocumentBuilder().parse(InputSource(StringReader(xml)))
        val out = LinkedHashMap<String, Res>()
        val nodes = doc.documentElement.childNodes
        for (i in 0 until nodes.length) {
            val e = nodes.item(i) as? Element ?: continue
            val name = e.getAttribute("name")
            val translatable = e.getAttribute("translatable") != "false"
            when (e.tagName) {
                "string" -> out[name] = Res(name, translatable, false, mapOf("" to e.textContent))
                "plurals" -> {
                    val items = LinkedHashMap<String, String>()
                    val kids = e.getElementsByTagName("item")
                    for (j in 0 until kids.length) {
                        val item = kids.item(j) as Element
                        items[item.getAttribute("quantity")] = item.textContent
                    }
                    out[name] = Res(name, translatable, true, items)
                }
            }
        }
        return out
    }

    /** %1$s, %2$d, %s, %d, %.1f…; "%%" is a percent sign and is not a marker. */
    private val marker = Regex("%(?:(\\d+)\\$)?[-#+ 0,(]*\\d*(?:\\.\\d+)?([a-zA-Z%])")

    /** The markers of a text as "index:conversion"; an unnumbered one has index 0. */
    fun markers(text: String): List<String> = marker.findAll(text)
        .filter { it.groupValues[2] != "%" && it.groupValues[2] != "n" }
        .map { "${it.groupValues[1].ifEmpty { "0" }}:${it.groupValues[2]}" }
        .toList().sorted()

    /** A text AAPT2 would break or change silently: a bare ' or ", or a first character that makes it a reference. */
    fun escapingProblem(text: String): String? {
        var i = 0
        while (i < text.length) {
            val c = text[i]
            if (c == '\\') { i += 2; continue }
            if (c == '\'') return "an apostrophe without a backslash (write \\')"
            if (c == '"') return "a double quote without a backslash (write \\\")"
            i++
        }
        if (text.startsWith("@") || text.startsWith("?")) return "starts with @ or ?, which Android reads as a reference (write \\@ or \\?)"
        return null
    }

    /** The same text in both languages on purpose (a name, a symbol, a word that is the same). Each one says why. */
    val sameInBoth = mapOf(
        "perm_deny" to "\"No\" is the same word in both languages",
    )

    fun problems(en: Map<String, Res>, es: Map<String, Res>): List<String> {
        val out = mutableListOf<String>()
        for ((name, e) in en) {
            if (!e.translatable) continue
            val s = es[name]
            if (s == null) { out += "$name: missing in values-es"; continue }
            if (s.plural != e.plural) { out += "$name: a plurals in one language and a string in the other"; continue }
            if (e.texts.keys != s.texts.keys) out += "$name: the quantities differ: en ${e.texts.keys}, es ${s.texts.keys}"
            if (e.plural) for ((lang, r) in listOf("en" to e, "es" to s)) {
                if ("one" !in r.texts || "other" !in r.texts) out += "$name: $lang needs one and other"
            }
            for ((q, et) in e.texts) {
                val st = s.texts[q] ?: continue
                val label = if (e.plural) "$name[$q]" else name
                if (et.isBlank()) out += "$label: empty in values"
                if (st.isBlank()) out += "$label: empty in values-es"
                val me = markers(et)
                val ms = markers(st)
                if (me.size > 1 && me.any { it.startsWith("0:") }) out += "$label: more than one marker needs positions (%1\$s, %2\$d) in values"
                if (ms.size > 1 && ms.any { it.startsWith("0:") }) out += "$label: more than one marker needs positions (%1\$s, %2\$d) in values-es"
                if (me != ms) out += "$label: the markers differ: values $me, values-es $ms"
                if (et == st && name !in sameInBoth && Regex("\\p{L}{2,}").containsMatchIn(et)) out += "$label: the same text in both languages (untranslated?); list it in sameInBoth with a reason if it is right"
                escapingProblem(et)?.let { out += "$label (values): $it" }
                escapingProblem(st)?.let { out += "$label (values-es): $it" }
            }
        }
        for ((name, s) in es) {
            if (name !in en) out += "$name: in values-es but not in values"
            else if (!s.translatable && en[name]?.translatable == true) out += "$name: translatable=\"false\" only in values-es"
        }
        return out
    }
}

class StringsParityTest {
    private fun res(dir: String): Map<String, Res> =
        StringsCheck.parse(File(System.getProperty("adbuddy.resDir"), "$dir/strings.xml").readText())

    @Test fun everyStringIsInBothLanguagesWithTheSameMarkersAndPlurals() {
        val problems = StringsCheck.problems(res("values"), res("values-es"))
        assertTrue("strings.xml and values-es/strings.xml disagree:\n" + problems.joinToString("\n"), problems.isEmpty())
    }

    // The lock-screen texts are public (spec §4): neither language gives them an argument, so nothing private can be put in them.
    @Test fun theLockScreenTextsTakeNoArgumentsInAnyLanguage() {
        for (dir in listOf("values", "values-es")) {
            val strings = res(dir)
            for (name in listOf("notif_public_connected", "notif_done_title")) {
                val s = strings[name]
                assertTrue("$dir: $name is missing", s != null)
                for ((quantity, text) in s!!.texts) {
                    assertEquals("$dir: $name[$quantity] is shown on the lock screen and takes no marker", emptyList<String>(), StringsCheck.markers(text))
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Test fun theCheckSeesWhatItShould() {
        fun p(en: String, es: String) = StringsCheck.problems(StringsCheck.parse("<resources>$en</resources>"), StringsCheck.parse("<resources>$es</resources>"))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), p("<string name=\"a\">Hello %1\$s</string>", "<string name=\"a\">Hola %1\$s</string>"))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), p("<string name=\"a\" translatable=\"false\">ADBuddy</string>", ""))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), p("<string name=\"a\">100%% of %1\$s</string>", "<string name=\"a\">100%% de %1\$s</string>"))
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), p("<string name=\"a\">%1\$s of %2\$s</string>", "<string name=\"a\">%2\$s de %1\$s</string>")) // reordering is fine
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">x</string>", "").single().contains("missing in values-es"))
        assertTrue(p("", "<string name=\"a\">x</string>").single().contains("not in values"))
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">Hello %1\$s</string>", "<string name=\"a\">Hola %1\$d</string>").single().contains("markers differ"))
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">Hello %1\$s</string>", "<string name=\"a\">Hola</string>").single().contains("markers differ"))
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">%s of %s</string>", "<string name=\"a\">%s de %s</string>").any { it.contains("needs positions") })
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">Hello</string>", "<string name=\"a\"> </string>").any { it.contains("empty in values-es") })
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">Same words</string>", "<string name=\"a\">Same words</string>").single().contains("untranslated"))
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">Don't</string>", "<string name=\"a\">No</string>").any { it.contains("apostrophe") })
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), p("<string name=\"a\">Don\\'t</string>", "<string name=\"a\">No lo hagas</string>"))
        assertTrue(p("<string name=\"a\">@home</string>", "<string name=\"a\">hogar</string>").any { it.contains("reference") })
        val plural = "<plurals name=\"n\"><item quantity=\"one\">%1\$d file</item><item quantity=\"other\">%1\$d files</item></plurals>"
        val pluralEs = "<plurals name=\"n\"><item quantity=\"one\">%1\$d fichero</item><item quantity=\"other\">%1\$d ficheros</item></plurals>"
        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), p(plural, pluralEs))
        assertTrue(p(plural, "<plurals name=\"n\"><item quantity=\"other\">%1\$d ficheros</item></plurals>").any { it.contains("quantities differ") })
        assertTrue(p("<plurals name=\"n\"><item quantity=\"other\">%1\$d files</item></plurals>", "<plurals name=\"n\"><item quantity=\"other\">%1\$d ficheros</item></plurals>").any { it.contains("needs one and other") })
        assertTrue(p(plural, "<string name=\"n\">x</string>").single().contains("plurals in one language"))
    }
}
```

**Criterios de aceptación de A1**
1. `testDebugUnitTest` y `lintDebug` verdes sobre el árbol; los tests existentes sin tocar.
2. Los tres cambios del paso 5 hacen fallar el test que dicen, y revertidos vuelven al verde.
3. El lint usa `error += …` (no `checkOnly`, que apagaría el resto: p. ej. `BidiSpoofing`, `ExportedContentProvider`) y no sube `MissingQuantity` (en es pide `many`, que es para millones).

## Tarea A2: Arreglos de la app (errores por código, horas y tamaños, idioma por app, canales)

**Árbol:** `android/` · **Depende de:** A1 · **Revisor:** `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` (la app muestra texto de errores, notificaciones y tarjetas) · **Commit:** `feat(android): errores por codigo, horas y tamanos segun el idioma, idioma por app y canales que se repintan`

**Qué cambia (spec §5.3):**
1. **`ErrorText`** (`bridge/ErrorText.kt`): `ErrorReply` → texto por `code` con 13 cadenas `error_*` (12 `ErrorCode` + `error_unknown`). Para `failed`, `busy`, `unavailable`, `too_large` y un código desconocido se añade el `text` del host entre paréntesis (≤ 200 caracteres, sin partir un par sustituto; un código desconocido sin `text` muestra el código, ≤ 64) porque el host explica la causa accionable; para el resto se ignora. `error_unavailable` es neutro («no está disponible ahora»): a veces falta algo en el móvil. Los **7** sitios que enseñaban `ErrorReply.text` reciben un `ErrorTexts` (`(ErrorReply) -> String`): `ChatController` (2), `Captures`, `Downloads`, `Drafts`/`UploadFailed.display` (que cubre `AdbuddyApp.attachUri`) y `RemoteCard`. Los valores por defecto de los constructores son los de hoy (`ErrorText.plain`, `ErrorText.hostText`), así que **ningún test existente cambia**; el parámetro nuevo va **antes** del lambda final (`newKey`, `newId`) para no robarle el *trailing lambda* a los tests.
2. **Horas y tamaños:** `Formats.time` (`DateTimeFormatter.ofLocalizedTime`, `MEDIUM` en la notificación de confirmación, `SHORT` en `RemoteCard`) y `Formatter.formatShortFileSize` en lugar de `sizeText`.
3. **Idioma por app** (Android 13+): `res/xml/locales_config.xml` y `android:localeConfig`; **`androidResources.localeFilters = en, es`** (APK de release −4,2 %); ayudante `LanguageSettings` (sin pantalla ni fila: la pone M5c).
4. **Canales y notificación al cambiar de idioma:** `AdbuddyApp.onConfigurationChanged` vuelve a crear los canales y `BridgeService.onConfigurationChanged` repinta su notificación fija (sin receptor de `ACTION_LOCALE_CHANGED` y sin permisos nuevos; es más simple que lo que decía la spec, que pedía a `BridgeService` un repintado desde `AdbuddyApp`).
5. **Hallazgo de A1:** `ActivityScreen` deja de enseñar la palabra de estado cruda del diario (`activity_status_*` con respaldo en la palabra tal cual) y sus dos líneas pasan a recursos (`activity_entry`, `activity_inverse`); salen las dos excepciones de A1.
6. **`LocalesTest`** (instrumentado): idioma `en`, `es`, `es-MX`/`es-419` y otro cualquiera (→ inglés) y los 13 textos de error. **El workflow `emulator.yml` no ejecuta `androidTest`** (solo el oráculo del clasificador): `android.yml` pasa a compilarlo (`assembleDebugAndroidTest`) y la ejecución en un emulador queda en «Validaciones manuales pendientes».
7. **Lint:** Compose avisa de que `LocalContext.current.getString` no se entera de los cambios de configuración: en `RemoteCard` se usa `LocalResources.current`.

**Ficheros:** crear `bridge/ErrorText.kt`, `ui/Formats.kt`, `ui/LanguageSettings.kt`, `res/xml/locales_config.xml` y los tests `ErrorTextTest`, `FormatsTest`, `LanguageSettingsTest`, `androidTest/…/LocalesTest.kt`; reemplazar `i18n-exceptions.txt` (queda solo con sus comentarios); modificar `ChatController.kt`, `Captures.kt`, `Downloads.kt`, `Drafts.kt`, `Uploader.kt`, `AdbuddyApp.kt`, `RemoteCard.kt`, `ConfirmNotifier.kt`, `ChatParts.kt`, `ActivityScreen.kt`, `BridgeService.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml`, `strings.xml` (+23 cadenas: 13 `error_*`, 2 `settings_language*`, 6 `activity_status_*`, 2 de las líneas del diario), `values-es/strings.xml`, `StringsParityTest.kt`, `build.gradle.kts`, `.github/workflows/android.yml`.

**Crear `android/app/src/androidTest/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/LocalesTest.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy

import android.content.Context
import android.content.res.Configuration
import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
import java.util.Locale
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith

/** The strings in the language of a context, as Android resolves them (M6c spec 5.4): the real resources, on a device or emulator. */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class LocalesTest {
    private val target = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().targetContext

    private fun inLanguage(tag: String): Context {
        val config = Configuration(target.resources.configuration)
        config.setLocale(Locale.forLanguageTag(tag))
        return target.createConfigurationContext(config)
    }

    @Test fun english() {
        val c = inLanguage("en")
        assertEquals("The agent wants to: x", c.getString(R.string.confirm_title, "x"))
        assertEquals("Working: x", c.getString(R.string.notif_working_on, "x"))
        assertEquals("1 more waiting", c.resources.getQuantityString(R.plurals.perm_more, 1, 1))
        assertEquals("3 more waiting", c.resources.getQuantityString(R.plurals.perm_more, 3, 3))
    }

    @Test fun spanish() {
        val c = inLanguage("es")
        assertEquals("El agente quiere: x", c.getString(R.string.confirm_title, "x"))
        assertEquals("Trabajando: x", c.getString(R.string.notif_working_on, "x"))
        assertEquals("1 más esperando", c.resources.getQuantityString(R.plurals.perm_more, 1, 1))
        assertEquals("3 más esperando", c.resources.getQuantityString(R.plurals.perm_more, 3, 3))
    }

    @Test fun spanishOfOtherCountriesIsSpanish() {
        assertEquals("Conectado", inLanguage("es-MX").getString(R.string.notif_public_connected))
        assertEquals("Conectado", inLanguage("es-419").getString(R.string.notif_public_connected))
    }

    @Test fun anyOtherLanguageIsEnglish() {
        assertEquals("Connected", inLanguage("fr").getString(R.string.notif_public_connected))
        assertEquals("Connected", inLanguage("de-DE").getString(R.string.notif_public_connected))
    }

    @Test fun everyErrorCodeHasATextInBothLanguages() {
        for (tag in listOf("en", "es")) {
            val c = inLanguage(tag)
            for (id in listOf(R.string.error_unsupported, R.string.error_invalid, R.string.error_not_found, R.string.error_no_agent, R.string.error_busy,
                R.string.error_too_large, R.string.error_checksum, R.string.error_failed, R.string.error_unavailable, R.string.error_protected,
                R.string.error_expired, R.string.error_ambiguous, R.string.error_unknown)) {
                assertTrue("$tag: empty text for ${c.resources.getResourceEntryName(id)}", c.getString(id).isNotBlank())
            }
        }
    }
}
```

**Crear `android/app/src/main/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/bridge/ErrorText.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.bridge

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import tech.hybriq.adbuddy.R

/** What the app shows of an [ErrorReply]: the host's `text` is English by protocol and detail for the agent and the log. */
typealias ErrorTexts = (ErrorReply) -> String

/**
 * The text of an ErrorReply in the language of the phone, by its `code` (protocol spec §4.1 and §8.1; M6c ruling 4).
 * Every ErrorCode has its error_<code> string (StringsParityTest checks it), and a code this app does not know shows
 * error_unknown with the host's text after it.
 */
object ErrorText {
    /** The way the controllers read an error when they have no Context (the tests'): the host's text, else the code. */
    val plain: ErrorTexts = { it.text.ifEmpty { it.code } }

    /** Only what the host wrote, maybe empty: the uploads' (the screen words the empty case itself). */
    val hostText: ErrorTexts = { it.text }

    @StringRes
    fun resFor(code: String): Int = when (code) {
        ErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED -> R.string.error_unsupported
        ErrorCode.INVALID -> R.string.error_invalid
        ErrorCode.NOT_FOUND -> R.string.error_not_found
        ErrorCode.NO_AGENT -> R.string.error_no_agent
        ErrorCode.BUSY -> R.string.error_busy
        ErrorCode.TOO_LARGE -> R.string.error_too_large
        ErrorCode.CHECKSUM -> R.string.error_checksum
        ErrorCode.FAILED -> R.string.error_failed
        ErrorCode.UNAVAILABLE -> R.string.error_unavailable
        ErrorCode.PROTECTED -> R.string.error_protected
        ErrorCode.EXPIRED -> R.string.error_expired
        ErrorCode.AMBIGUOUS -> R.string.error_ambiguous
        else -> R.string.error_unknown
    }

    /** The text for [e], with [getString] resolving the string in the language in force. For `failed` and an unknown code the host's own text follows, in parentheses and at most 200 characters. */
    fun text(getString: (Int) -> String, e: ErrorReply): String {
        val own = getString(resFor(e.code))
        val detail = e.text.trim().take(200)
        val withDetail = e.code == ErrorCode.FAILED || resFor(e.code) == R.string.error_unknown
        return if (withDetail && detail.isNotEmpty()) "$own ($detail)" else own
    }
}
```

**Crear `android/app/src/main/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/ui/Formats.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.ui

import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.time.format.FormatStyle
import java.util.Locale

/** Times as the language in force writes them: "14:05:09" in Spanish, "2:05:09 PM" in English. */
object Formats {
    fun time(epochMs: Long, style: FormatStyle, zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault(), locale: Locale = Locale.getDefault()): String =
        DateTimeFormatter.ofLocalizedTime(style).withLocale(locale).withZone(zone).format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(epochMs))
}
```

**Crear `android/app/src/main/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/ui/LanguageSettings.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.ui

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.Settings

/**
 * Opens the system's page for this app's language (Android 13 and later: Settings, Apps, ADBuddy, Language; the
 * languages are the ones in res/xml/locales_config.xml). Below Android 13 the app follows the language of the system
 * and there is nothing to open. M5c puts it behind the language row of its Settings screen.
 */
object LanguageSettings {
    fun available(sdkInt: Int = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT): Boolean = sdkInt >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU

    /** True when the page was opened. */
    fun open(context: Context): Boolean {
        if (!available()) return false
        val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_APP_LOCALE_SETTINGS, Uri.fromParts("package", context.packageName, null))
            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        return try {
            context.startActivity(intent)
            true
        } catch (e: android.content.ActivityNotFoundException) {
            false
        }
    }
}
```

**Crear `android/app/src/test/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/bridge/ErrorTextTest.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.bridge

import java.lang.reflect.Modifier
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import tech.hybriq.adbuddy.R

class ErrorTextTest {
    private val str: (Int) -> String = { "s$it" }

    private fun codes() = ErrorCode::class.java.declaredFields
        .filter { Modifier.isStatic(it.modifiers) && it.type == String::class.java }
        .map { it.get(null) as String }

    @Test fun everyCodeOfTheProtocolHasItsOwnString() {
        val codes = codes()
        assertTrue("ErrorCode changed shape: $codes", codes.size >= 12)
        val ids = codes.map { ErrorText.resFor(it) }
        assertTrue("a code with no string of its own: $codes", ids.none { it == R.string.error_unknown })
        assertEquals("two codes share a string", ids.size, ids.toSet().size)
    }

    @Test fun anUnknownCodeIsErrorUnknown() {
        assertEquals(R.string.error_unknown, ErrorText.resFor("a_code_of_the_future"))
        assertEquals(R.string.error_unknown, ErrorText.resFor(""))
    }

    @Test fun theHostTextIsShownOnlyForFailedAndUnknownCodes() {
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_failed} (disk full)", ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply(ErrorCode.FAILED, "disk full")))
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_unknown} (new thing)", ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply("new_code", "new thing")))
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_busy}", ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply(ErrorCode.BUSY, "The agent is busy")))
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_too_large}", ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply(ErrorCode.TOO_LARGE, "x")))
    }

    @Test fun anEmptyHostTextAddsNothingAndALongOneIsCut() {
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_failed}", ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply(ErrorCode.FAILED, "")))
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_failed}", ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply(ErrorCode.FAILED, "   ")))
        val long = ErrorText.text(str, ErrorReply(ErrorCode.FAILED, "x".repeat(500)))
        assertEquals("s${R.string.error_failed} (${"x".repeat(200)})", long)
    }

    @Test fun theControllersDefaultsKeepTheirOldReading() {
        assertEquals("boom", ErrorText.plain(ErrorReply("failed", "boom")))
        assertEquals("failed", ErrorText.plain(ErrorReply("failed", "")))
        assertEquals("", ErrorText.hostText(ErrorReply("failed", "")))
        assertNotEquals("", ErrorText.hostText(ErrorReply("failed", "boom")))
    }
}
```

**Crear `android/app/src/test/kotlin/tech/hybriq/adbuddy/ui/FormatsTest.kt`:**

```kotlin
package tech.hybriq.adbuddy.ui

import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneOffset
import java.time.format.FormatStyle
import java.util.Locale
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class FormatsTest {
    private val at = Instant.parse("2026-10-02T14:05:09Z").toEpochMilli()

    /** Newer JDKs put a narrow no-break space before PM: the same text for the person. */
    private fun plain(s: String) = s.replace(' ', ' ').replace(' ', ' ')

    @Test fun spanishWritesTheHourOf24() {
        assertEquals("14:05:09", plain(Formats.time(at, FormatStyle.MEDIUM, ZoneOffset.UTC, Locale.forLanguageTag("es-ES"))))
        assertEquals("14:05", plain(Formats.time(at, FormatStyle.SHORT, ZoneOffset.UTC, Locale.forLanguageTag("es"))))
    }

    @Test fun englishWritesItsOwnWay() {
        assertEquals("2:05:09 PM", plain(Formats.time(at, FormatStyle.MEDIUM, ZoneOffset.UTC, Locale.US)))
        assertEquals("2:05 PM", plain(Formats.time(at, FormatStyle.SHORT, ZoneOffset.UTC, Locale.US)))
    }

    @Test fun theZoneIsTheOneAsked() {
        assertEquals("16:05:09", plain(Formats.time(at, FormatStyle.MEDIUM, ZoneOffset.ofHours(2), Locale.forLanguageTag("es-ES"))))
    }
}
```

```kotlin
    fun display(errorText: ErrorTexts): String = if (code == CONNECTION) "" else errorText(ErrorReply(code, text))

    companion object {
```

```kotlin
    override fun onConfigurationChanged(newConfig: Configuration) {
```

```kotlin
private val TIME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm")

/** expiresAt is in the host's clock; HostClock moves it to the phone's before it is shown as local time. */
private fun localTime(hostInstant: Instant, clock: HostClock): String =
    TIME.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(clock.toLocal(hostInstant.toEpochMilli())).atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()))
```

```kotlin
private fun sizeText(n: Long): String = when {
    n >= 1024L * 1024 -> "%.1f MB".format(n / 1048576.0)
    n >= 1024 -> "%d KB".format(n / 1024)
    else -> "$n B"
}
```

```kotlin
/** The journal's status word (running, ok, error, denied, blocked, timeout) in the language in force; one this app does not know shows as it came. */
@Composable
private fun statusText(status: String): String = when (status) {
    "running" -> stringResource(R.string.activity_status_running)
    "ok" -> stringResource(R.string.activity_status_ok)
    "error" -> stringResource(R.string.activity_status_error)
    "denied" -> stringResource(R.string.activity_status_denied)
    "blocked" -> stringResource(R.string.activity_status_blocked)
    "timeout" -> stringResource(R.string.activity_status_timeout)
    else -> status
}

    "green" -> "🟢"
```

Esperado: verde, **450 tests** (440 + 10: `ErrorTextTest` 5, `FormatsTest` 3, `LanguageSettingsTest` 1, `StringsParityTest` +1), lint sin errores, y los dos APK (`app-debug.apk`, `app-debug-androidTest.apk`). `FormatsTest` normaliza el espacio estrecho antes de `PM` (JDK 20+).

- [ ] **Paso 5: Medir el APK (spec §5.1).** Con `localeFilters` puesto: `gradle :app:assembleRelease` y anota el tamaño de `app/build/outputs/apk/release/*.apk`; comenta la línea `localeFilters += …`, repite y compara (medido: 3 124 455 con, 3 259 787 sin: −135 332 bytes, −4,2 %; el APK de debug no cambia). Deja `localeFilters` puesto. Apunta las dos cifras en el ledger. Si AGP rechazara `localeFilters`, se omite (coste: un APK algo mayor).

- [ ] **Paso 6: Instrumentado en el emulador** (permitido: no es el móvil del usuario). `scripts/emulator.sh start` imprime las variables; con `ANDROID_ADB_SERVER_PORT=5039` y `ANDROID_SERIAL=emulator-5580`: `gradle :app:connectedDebugAndroidTest`. Esperado: `LocalesTest` (5 tests) y `TokenProviderTest` verdes. Si el emulador no arranca en esta máquina, anótalo y deja la ejecución en «Validaciones manuales pendientes» (el test **compila** en la CI).

- [ ] **Paso 7: Commit.**

```bash
git add android/app/src/main android/app/src/test android/app/src/androidTest android/app/build.gradle.kts .github/workflows/android.yml
git commit -m "feat(android): errores por codigo, horas y tamanos segun el idioma, idioma por app y canales que se repintan" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

**Criterios de aceptación de A2**
1. `testDebugUnitTest`, `lintDebug`, `assembleDebug`, `assembleDebugAndroidTest` verdes; 450 tests; ningún test existente modificado.
2. Para **cada** constante de `ErrorCode` hay `error_<code>` en los dos idiomas (`StringsParityTest` por reflexión) y `error_unknown`; un código desconocido sale con `error_unknown` más el texto del host.
3. `i18n-exceptions.txt` queda sin entradas; `NoHardcodedTextTest` verde sin ellas.
4. Hora: «14:05:09» en es y «2:05:09 PM» en en (`FormatsTest`); tamaño con `Formatter.formatShortFileSize`; ningún `Locale` ni `Configuration` manual en el código de producción.
5. `values-es` y `values` coinciden en claves, marcadores y plurales (`perm_more`); `locales_config` lista `en` y `es`; el manifest lleva `android:localeConfig`.
6. **Seguridad (revisor opus):** la tarjeta 🔴 y la de permiso no cambian (esta tarea no las toca); el texto público de la pantalla de bloqueo (`notif_public_connected`, `notif_done_title`) sigue sin argumentos; los 7 sitios de `ErrorReply.text` no enseñan inglés con la app en español.

---

## Tarea H4a: Razones bilingües (la parte de seguridad del clasificador)

**Árbol:** `host/` · **Depende de:** H0 · **Implementador:** `adbuddy-implementer`, con escalado a `adbuddy-implementer-opus` si se atasca (rondas 4-5 o BLOCKED) · **Revisor:** `adbuddy-reviewer-opus` (qué lee el usuario al decidir un 🔴) · **Commit:** `feat(classify): las razones de las confirmaciones son bilingües`

**Qué cambia (spec §4.1 y §6.5, con la medida real):** el `detail` de una pregunta 🔴 incluye las razones (`classify.Finding.Reason`, `precheck.Prepared.Reason`, las de `daemon/pcwrite.go`), hoy solo en inglés dentro de una frase en español. Pasan a ser `classify.Text{EN, ES}`:
- `Finding.Reason` y `PCWrite.Reason`, `checkResult.reason`, `unwrap`/`unwrapWords`, `pcTargets`, `specialReason`, `amSpecial`, las gramáticas de builtins y `adbAlwaysRed` pasan de `string` a `Text`; `redFinding`, `unanalysable`, `backgroundFinding`, `red` y `unsupported` reciben `Text`. **Un `string` ya no compila**: una razón nueva sin su `reason(...)` no se puede escribir.
- `reason("plantilla en inglés con {marcadores}", "marcador", valor…)` busca el español en la tabla `reasonsES` (clave = la plantilla inglesa) y rellena los dos idiomas igual. **179 plantillas** (183 llamadas).
- `classify.Join`, `classify.Same` (el mismo texto en los dos idiomas: una ruta, un error del sistema) y `classify.Cat` (concatena) los usan `precheck` y `daemon/pcwrite.go`, que construyen `Text{EN, ES}` a mano (7 + 9 textos).
- `daemon.go`: `reason` pasa a `classify.Text`; `detail` usa `reason.In(d.o.Lang)` pasado por `Printable`; la clave de «permitir durante la sesión» sigue siendo vacía si hay razón del PC o del móvil (no depende del idioma).
- **El diario no se toca:** `Finding.Reason` no se escribe en él (la spec §6.5 lo suponía; medido: solo entra en el `detail`). `Entry.Reason` sigue en inglés.

**Interfaces que produce:** `classify.Text.Empty() bool`, `classify.Join(a, b Text) Text` (une con «; »), `classify.Same(s string) Text`, `classify.Cat(parts ...Text) Text`; `precheck.Prepared.Reason classify.Text`; `(*Daemon).checkPCWrites(cwd, ws) classify.Text`.

**Controles:** `reasons_test.go` — `TestReasonsBilingual` (cada `reason("…")` del código tiene español en `reasonsES`, con los mismos `{marcadores}` y llenados con los mismos nombres; la tabla no tiene entradas muertas ni acabadas en punto), `TestTitlesBilingual` (las reglas YAML, los `Text{EN, ES}` de Go y los de `precheck` y `daemon`), `TestCorpusIsFullyInSpanish` (clasifica `habitual`, `m2-levels`, `raised` y todos los casos diferenciales: **ningún título ni razón de ningún 🔴 cae a inglés** y el `Detail("es")` no contiene la razón en inglés ni cambia de nº de líneas); `daemon/reasons_lang_test.go` — la misma pregunta en en y es tiene el mismo `RawCommand`, `Special` y `Key` (sin el directorio), con las razones de una regla, con un valor, del directorio del PC y del estado del móvil.

- [ ] **Paso 1: Herramienta.** Guarda `rewrite/main.go` del Apéndice A en una carpeta **fuera del repo** con un `go.mod` (`module rewrite`, `go 1.22`) y `go build -o rewrite.exe .`. Comprueba antes de seguir que `git status` está limpio y que `go test -count=1 -timeout 120s ./internal/classify/ ./internal/precheck/ ./internal/daemon/` pasa (línea base).

- [ ] **Paso 2: Pasada mecánica sobre `classify`** (envuelve en `reason(...)` los 226 sitios: argumentos de `red`, `redFinding`, `unanalysable`, `unsupported`, `backgroundFinding`, `bad`, `return` de las funciones de razón y valores de `adbAlwaysRed`; las concatenaciones se convierten en plantillas con `{marcadores}`):

```bash
cd host/internal/classify
/ruta/a/rewrite.exe .            # imprime: arith.go 6, builtins.go 16, checks.go 12, checks_change.go 19, checks_read.go 31, classify.go 7, shell.go 11, special.go 37, vars.go 4, word.go 7, wrappers.go 76
sed -i 's/reason("")/Text{}/g' $(ls *.go | grep -v _test)
```

- [ ] **Paso 3: Ficheros nuevos** (`reasons.go` con `reason`, `Join`, `Same`, `Cat`; `reasons_es.go` con las 179 traducciones; los tests).

**Crear `host/internal/classify/reasons.go`:**

```go
package classify

// reason builds the Text of a reason, the "why" that goes into the detail of a
// 🔴 question: the English template, whose {placeholders} are filled by the
// name/value pairs after it, and its Spanish from reasonsES, filled the same
// way. The Spanish of a template that is not in reasonsES is empty, which
// Text.In answers in English; TestReasonsBilingual makes that impossible in a
// build that passes its tests. A value is not translated and not escaped here:
// Result.Detail escapes the whole line (Printable).
func reason(en string, kv ...string) Text {
	t := Text{EN: en, ES: reasonsES[en]}
	if len(kv) == 0 {
		return t
	}
	vars := make(map[string]string, len(kv)/2)
	for i := 0; i+1 < len(kv); i += 2 {
		vars[kv[i]] = kv[i+1]
	}
	return render(t, vars)
}

// Empty reports whether t says nothing: no reason.
func (t Text) Empty() bool { return t.EN == "" }

// Join is two reasons in one line, either of which may be empty.
func Join(a, b Text) Text {
	switch {
	case b.Empty():
		return a
	case a.Empty():
		return b
	}
	return Text{EN: a.EN + "; " + b.EN, ES: a.ES + "; " + b.ES}
}

// Same is a Text that says the same in both languages: a path, a name, the
// message of an error of the system.
func Same(s string) Text { return Text{EN: s, ES: s} }

// Cat puts texts one after the other, in each language.
func Cat(parts ...Text) Text {
	var t Text
	for _, p := range parts {
		t.EN += p.EN
		t.ES += p.ES
	}
	return t
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/classify/reasons_es.go`:**

```go
package classify

// reasonsES is the Spanish of every reason of the classifier, by the English
// template reason("…") is called with. A reason says why a command needs a
// confirmation; it is read between parentheses after the title: lower case,
// no final period, "móvil" and "PC", and the names of commands, options and
// settings untouched. A {placeholder} is filled with the same value in both
// languages. Keep the entries in the order of the files they come from.
var reasonsES = map[string]string{
	// arith.go
	"[ without a closing ]":                                               "[ sin el ] de cierre",
	"a test expression ADBuddy cannot read":                               "una expresión de test que ADBuddy no sabe leer",
	"a word of test only known on the phone could be an integer operator": "una palabra de test que solo se conoce en el móvil podría ser un operador de enteros",
	"a word of test that may split into operators on the phone":           "una palabra de test que en el móvil puede dividirse en operadores",
	"arithmetic on a value not proven to be a number":                     "aritmética sobre un valor que no se ha comprobado que sea un número",
```

**Crear `host/internal/classify/reasons_test.go`:**

```go
package classify

import (
	"go/ast"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

var placeholderRE = regexp.MustCompile(`\{[a-z][a-z0-9_]*\}`)

// placeholders lists the {names} of a template, sorted. The braces of the
// shell in a template ("${ …;}") are not placeholders.
func placeholders(s string) []string {
	found := placeholderRE.FindAllString(s, -1)
	sort.Strings(found)
	return found
}

func samePlaceholders(a, b string) bool {
	return strings.Join(placeholders(a), " ") == strings.Join(placeholders(b), " ")
}

// sources parses the non-test Go files of the package.
func sources(t *testing.T) (*token.FileSet, map[string]*ast.File) {
	t.Helper()
	return sourcesIn(t, ".")
}

// sourcesIn parses the non-test Go files of dir.
func sourcesIn(t *testing.T, dir string) (*token.FileSet, map[string]*ast.File) {
	t.Helper()
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	files := map[string]*ast.File{}
	names, err := filepath.Glob(filepath.Join(dir, "*.go"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, name := range names {
		if strings.HasSuffix(name, "_test.go") {
			continue
		}
		f, err := parser.ParseFile(fset, name, nil, 0)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		files[name] = f
	}
	return fset, files
}

func unquote(e ast.Expr) (string, bool) {
	lit, ok := e.(*ast.BasicLit)
	if !ok || lit.Kind != token.STRING {
		return "", false
	}
	s, err := strconv.Unquote(lit.Value)
	return s, err == nil
}

// reasonCall is one reason("template", "name", value…) in the code.
type reasonCall struct {
	template string
	names    []string // the placeholders the call fills
	where    string
}

func reasonCalls(t *testing.T) (calls []reasonCall, dynamic []string) {
	t.Helper()
	fset, files := sources(t)
	for _, f := range files {
		ast.Inspect(f, func(n ast.Node) bool {
			c, ok := n.(*ast.CallExpr)
			if !ok {
				return true
			}
			if id, ok := c.Fun.(*ast.Ident); !ok || id.Name != "reason" || len(c.Args) == 0 {
				return true
			}
			where := fset.Position(c.Pos()).String()
			tpl, ok := unquote(c.Args[0])
			if !ok {
				dynamic = append(dynamic, where)
				return true
			}
			rc := reasonCall{template: tpl, where: where}
			for i := 1; i < len(c.Args); i += 2 {
				if name, ok := unquote(c.Args[i]); ok {
					rc.names = append(rc.names, "{"+name+"}")
				}
			}
			sort.Strings(rc.names)
			calls = append(calls, rc)
			return true
		})
	}
	return calls, dynamic
}

// Every reason the code can give has its Spanish, with the same placeholders,
// and the Spanish table has nothing the code does not use.
func TestReasonsBilingual(t *testing.T) {
	calls, dynamic := reasonCalls(t)
	for _, w := range dynamic {
		t.Errorf("%s: reason() with a template that is not a literal", w)
	}
	if len(calls) < 150 {
		t.Fatalf("only %d reason() calls found: did the scan break?", len(calls))
	}
	used := map[string]bool{}
	for _, c := range calls {
		used[c.template] = true
		es, ok := reasonsES[c.template]
		switch {
		case !ok:
			t.Errorf("%s: %q has no Spanish in reasonsES", c.where, c.template)
			continue
		case es == "" || es == c.template:
			t.Errorf("%s: the Spanish of %q is empty or the same text", c.where, c.template)
		case !samePlaceholders(c.template, es):
			t.Errorf("%s: %q and its Spanish %q have different {placeholders}", c.where, c.template, es)
		}
		if got, want := strings.Join(c.names, " "), strings.Join(placeholders(c.template), " "); got != want {
			t.Errorf("%s: %q is filled with %q but names %q", c.where, c.template, got, want)
		}
	}
	for k, es := range reasonsES {
		if !used[k] {
			t.Errorf("reasonsES has %q and no reason() call uses it", k)
		}
		if es != strings.TrimSpace(es) || strings.HasSuffix(es, ".") {
			t.Errorf("%q: the Spanish must have no spaces around it and no final period: %q", k, es)
		}
	}
}

// The rules (YAML) and the Go texts (Text{EN, ES}) of titles are bilingual too.
func TestTitlesBilingual(t *testing.T) {
	rules, err := builtinRules()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(rules) < 150 {
		t.Fatalf("%d built-in rules: did the YAML change shape?", len(rules))
	}
	for _, r := range rules {
		switch {
		case r.Title.ES == "":
			t.Errorf("rule %s has no Spanish title", r.ID)
		case !samePlaceholders(r.Title.EN, r.Title.ES):
			t.Errorf("rule %s: the title and its Spanish have different {placeholders}: %q / %q", r.ID, r.Title.EN, r.Title.ES)
		}
	}
	// The texts of the other packages that raise a question (the reasons of
	// the daemon and of the prechecks) are Text literals too.
	found := 0
	for _, dir := range []string{".", "../precheck", "../daemon"} {
		found += textLiteralsAreBilingual(t, dir)
	}
	if found < 30 {
		t.Fatalf("only %d Text literals found: did the scan break?", found)
	}
}

// textLiteralsAreBilingual checks every Text{EN: …, ES: …} (classify.Text{…}
// outside the package) of the Go files of dir and returns how many it found.
func textLiteralsAreBilingual(t *testing.T, dir string) int {
	t.Helper()
	fset, files := sourcesIn(t, dir)
	found := 0
	for _, f := range files {
		ast.Inspect(f, func(n ast.Node) bool {
			cl, ok := n.(*ast.CompositeLit)
			if !ok {
				return true
			}
			switch ty := cl.Type.(type) {
			case *ast.Ident:
				if ty.Name != "Text" {
					return true
				}
			case *ast.SelectorExpr:
				if ty.Sel.Name != "Text" {
					return true
				}
			default:
				return true
			}
			var en, es string
			var hasEN, hasES bool
			for _, el := range cl.Elts {
				kv, ok := el.(*ast.KeyValueExpr)
				if !ok {
					continue
				}
				key, _ := kv.Key.(*ast.Ident)
				if key == nil {
					continue
				}
				switch key.Name {
				case "EN":
					en, hasEN = literalPart(kv.Value)
				case "ES":
					es, hasES = literalPart(kv.Value)
				}
			}
			if !hasEN {
				return true
			}
			found++
			where := fset.Position(cl.Pos()).String()
			switch {
			case !hasES || es == "":
				t.Errorf("%s: Text{EN: %q} has no Spanish", where, en)
			case !samePlaceholders(en, es):
				t.Errorf("%s: %q and %q have different {placeholders}", where, en, es)
			}
			return true
		})
	}
	return found
}

// literalPart is the literal text of a string expression: a literal, or a
// concatenation of literals and values, whose values are left out.
func literalPart(e ast.Expr) (string, bool) {
	if s, ok := unquote(e); ok {
		return s, true
	}
	if b, ok := e.(*ast.BinaryExpr); ok && b.Op == token.ADD {
		x, okx := literalPart(b.X)
		y, oky := literalPart(b.Y)
		if okx || oky {
			return x + y, true
		}
	}
	return "", false
}

func TestReasonAndJoinReason(t *testing.T) {
	r := reason("unknown sed option {v}", "v", "-x")
	if r.EN != "unknown sed option -x" || r.ES != "opción de sed desconocida: -x" {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v", r)
	}
	if r := reason("a template that is not in the table"); r.EN != "a template that is not in the table" || r.ES != "" {
		t.Fatalf("an unknown template must have no Spanish (Text.In answers English): %+v", r)
	}
	if got := reason("a template that is not in the table").In("es"); got != "a template that is not in the table" {
		t.Fatalf("In(es) must fall back to English, never empty: %q", got)
	}
	if !(Text{}).Empty() || r.Empty() {
		t.Fatal("Empty")
	}
	j := Join(reason("restarts adbd"), reason("changes how adbd runs"))
	if j.EN != "restarts adbd; changes how adbd runs" || j.ES != "reinicia adbd; cambia cómo se ejecuta adbd" {
		t.Fatalf("join: %+v", j)
	}
	if j := Join(Text{}, reason("restarts adbd")); j.EN != "restarts adbd" {
		t.Fatalf("join with an empty first: %+v", j)
	}
	if j := Join(reason("restarts adbd"), Text{}); j.EN != "restarts adbd" {
		t.Fatalf("join with an empty second: %+v", j)
	}
}

// corpusResults classifies every case of every corpus, the generated
// differential cases included: the largest body of commands the classifier
// has been checked against.
func corpusResults(t *testing.T) []Result {
	t.Helper()
	c := mustNew(t, "")
	var out []Result
	for _, name := range []string{"habitual.jsonl", "m2-levels.jsonl", "raised.jsonl"} {
		for _, k := range loadCorpus(t, name) {
			if r, ok := safeClassify(t, c, k.argv()); ok {
				out = append(out, r)
			}
		}
	}
	for _, k := range differentialCases(t) {
		if r, ok := safeClassify(t, c, sh(withSentinel(k.Sh))); ok {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// Spec M6c §6.6 and §9.2: no title and no reason of any 🔴 of the corpora
// falls back to English, and the Spanish detail of a question has none of the
// English reasons in it.
func TestCorpusIsFullyInSpanish(t *testing.T) {
	var names []string
	for _, rs := range []map[string]string{reasonsES} {
		for k := range rs {
			for _, p := range placeholders(k) {
				names = append(names, p)
			}
		}
	}
	reds, reasons := 0, 0
	for _, r := range corpusResults(t) {
		if r.Level != Red {
			continue
		}
		reds++
		for _, f := range r.Findings {
			if f.Level != Red {
				continue
			}
			if f.Title.ES == "" || f.Title.ES == f.Title.EN {
				t.Errorf("%s: the title %q has no Spanish of its own (%q)", f.Rule, f.Title.EN, f.Title.ES)
			}
			if f.Reason.Empty() {
				continue
			}
			reasons++
			if f.Reason.ES == "" || f.Reason.ES == f.Reason.EN {
				t.Errorf("%s: the reason %q has no Spanish of its own (%q)", f.Rule, f.Reason.EN, f.Reason.ES)
			}
			for _, p := range names {
				if strings.Contains(f.Reason.EN, p) || strings.Contains(f.Reason.ES, p) {
					// A command that really has the text {value} would do this; none of the corpus does.
					t.Errorf("%s: the placeholder %s was not filled: %q / %q", f.Rule, p, f.Reason.EN, f.Reason.ES)
				}
			}
		}
		es, en := r.Detail("es"), r.Detail("en")
		if strings.Count(es, "\n") != strings.Count(en, "\n") {
			t.Errorf("the detail has %d lines in Spanish and %d in English:\n%s\n--\n%s", strings.Count(es, "\n")+1, strings.Count(en, "\n")+1, es, en)
		}
		for _, f := range r.Findings {
			if f.Level != Red || f.Reason.Empty() {
				continue
			}
			if !strings.Contains(es, Printable(f.Reason.ES)) {
				t.Errorf("the Spanish detail lacks the Spanish reason %q:\n%s", f.Reason.ES, es)
			}
			if f.Reason.EN != f.Reason.ES && strings.Contains(es, Printable(f.Reason.EN)) {
				t.Errorf("the Spanish detail has the English reason %q:\n%s", f.Reason.EN, es)
			}
		}
	}
	if reds < 1000 || reasons < 500 {
		t.Fatalf("the corpora gave %d 🔴 results and %d reasons: did they shrink?", reds, reasons)
	}
}
```

**Crear `host/internal/daemon/reasons_lang_test.go`:**

```go
package daemon

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/classify"
	"github.com/hybriqtech/adbuddy/host/internal/confirm"
)

// M6c §4.1 and §9.2: the question a person answers says the same in English
// and in Spanish: the reasons are in the language of the PC, whoever raised
// them (a rule, the agent's folder, the state of the phone), and everything
// that decides (the raw command, the level, special, the key of "allow
// during the session") is the same in both.

// askedIn runs argv from cwd in the given language, the user saying no, and
// returns the question that was asked.
func askedIn(t *testing.T, lang string, prepare func(*env), cwd string, argv ...string) confirm.Request {
	t.Helper()
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.d.o.Lang = lang
	if prepare != nil {
		prepare(e)
	}
	e.conf.decision = confirm.Deny
	if cwd == "" {
		cwd = t.TempDir()
	}
	_, exit := e.execIn(t, cwd, argv...)
	if exit.Code != ExitDenied || len(e.conf.asked) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%s %q: exit %+v asked %d", lang, argv, exit, len(e.conf.asked))
	}
	return e.conf.asked[0]
}

// withoutCwd is a key (rule, cwd and argv, NUL apart) without the cwd, which
// is a folder of its own in each run.
func withoutCwd(key string) string {
	parts := strings.Split(key, "\x00")
	if len(parts) < 3 {
		return key
	}
	return parts[0] + "\x00" + strings.Join(parts[2:], "\x00")
}

func TestTheQuestionDecidesTheSameInEnglishAndSpanish(t *testing.T) {
	outside := t.TempDir()
	for _, c := range []struct {
		name    string
		prepare func(*env)
		cwd     string
		argv    []string
		en, es  string // what the detail says in each language
		notInES string // English that must not be in the Spanish detail
		keyed   bool   // a question of a rule has a key; one that depends on state has none
	}{
		{name: "a reason of a rule", argv: []string{"shell", "sed", "-i", "s/a/b/", "/sdcard/x"},
			en: "sed -i edits files in place", es: "sed -i modifica los ficheros directamente", notInES: "edits files in place", keyed: true},
		{name: "a reason with a value", argv: []string{"shell", "sed", "-Q", "s/a/b/", "/sdcard/x"},
			en: "unknown sed option -Q", es: "opción de sed desconocida: -Q", notInES: "unknown sed option", keyed: true},
		{name: "the PC folder of adb pull", cwd: outside, argv: []string{"pull", "/sdcard/Download/x", "run.txt"},
			en: "writes outside the agent environment: " + outside, es: "escribe fuera del entorno del agente: " + outside, notInES: "writes outside"},
		{name: "the state of the phone", argv: []string{"shell", "pm", "uninstall", "--user", "0", "com.user"},
			prepare: func(e *env) { e.adb.shell["pm list packages -s com.user"] = "" },
			en:      "not a system app", es: "no es una app del sistema", notInES: "not a system app"},
	} {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			en := askedIn(t, "en", c.prepare, c.cwd, c.argv...)
			es := askedIn(t, "es", c.prepare, c.cwd, c.argv...)
			if !strings.Contains(en.Detail, c.en) {
				t.Errorf("English detail %q lacks %q", en.Detail, c.en)
			}
			if !strings.Contains(es.Detail, c.es) {
				t.Errorf("Spanish detail %q lacks %q", es.Detail, c.es)
			}
			if strings.Contains(es.Detail, c.notInES) {
				t.Errorf("Spanish detail %q has English: %q", es.Detail, c.notInES)
			}
			if en.Title == es.Title {
				t.Errorf("the title is the same in both languages: %q", en.Title)
			}
			if en.RawCommand != es.RawCommand || en.Special != es.Special || (en.Key != "") != c.keyed || (es.Key != "") != c.keyed {
				t.Errorf("what decides changed with the language: en %+v / es %+v", en, es)
			}
			if withoutCwd(en.Key) != withoutCwd(es.Key) {
				t.Errorf("the key of allow-during-the-session changed: %q / %q", en.Key, es.Key)
			}
		})
	}
}

// A reason that comes with a message of the system keeps it as it is in both
// languages, and the text around it is in each.
func TestPCWriteReasonWithASystemErrorIsInBothLanguages(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.d.o.Workspace = "/no/such/folder/for/this/test"
	got := e.d.checkPCWrites(t.TempDir(), []classify.PCWrite{{Cmd: "pull"}})
	if got.EN == "" || got.ES == "" || got.EN == got.ES || !strings.HasPrefix(got.ES, "pull escribe en el PC") {
		t.Fatalf("both languages must say their own thing: %+v", got)
	}
	if i := strings.Index(got.EN, ": "); i < 0 || !strings.HasSuffix(got.ES, got.EN[i+2:]) {
		t.Fatalf("the system's message must be the same in both: %+v", got)
	}
}
```

```go
func applySpecial(scope string, ws []Word, f *Finding) {
	if reason := specialReason(scope, ws, f.Level); reason != "" {
		f.Level = Red
		f.Special = true
		f.Op = nil
		setIdentity(scope, ws, f)
		if f.Reason == "" {
			f.Reason = reason
		} else {
			f.Reason = reason + "; " + f.Reason
		}
	}
}
```

```go
func applySpecial(scope string, ws []Word, f *Finding) {
	if why := specialReason(scope, ws, f.Level); !why.Empty() {
		f.Level = Red
		f.Special = true
		f.Op = nil
		setIdentity(scope, ws, f)
		f.Reason = Join(why, f.Reason)
	}
}
```

```go
// checkPCWrites returns why adb pull or adb bugreport would write outside the
// agent's environment or over its own files, or "" if every target stays
// inside. The working directory is the agent's (the shim's), so it is never
// trusted: it must itself be inside the environment. Paths are resolved
// through links where they exist, and compared without case on Windows.
func (d *Daemon) checkPCWrites(cwd string, ws []classify.PCWrite) string {
	for _, w := range ws {
		if why := d.checkPCWrite(cwd, w); why != "" {
			return why
		}
	}
	return ""
}

func (d *Daemon) checkPCWrite(cwd string, w classify.PCWrite) string {
	if w.Reason != "" {
		return w.Cmd + " " + w.Reason
	}
	if d.o.Workspace == "" {
		return w.Cmd + " writes on the PC and the agent environment is unknown"
	}
	root, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(d.o.Workspace)
	if err != nil {
		return w.Cmd + " writes on the PC and the agent environment cannot be read: " + err.Error()
	}
	outside := w.Cmd + " writes outside the agent environment: "
	own := w.Cmd + " writes over the agent environment's own files: "
	if cwd == "" || !filepath.IsAbs(cwd) {
		return outside + "(unknown working directory " + cwd + ")"
	}
	// adb runs in cwd as sent, and Linux and macOS resolve "out/.." on disk
	// (in the parent of out's target) while filepath.Clean just drops it.
	if cwd != filepath.Clean(cwd) || hasDotDot(cwd) {
		return outside + "(working directory not in clean form " + cwd + ")"
	}
	for _, t := range append([]string{"."}, w.Targets...) {
```

por

```go
// checkPCWrites returns why adb pull or adb bugreport would write outside the
// agent's environment or over its own files, or an empty Text if every target
// stays inside. The working directory is the agent's (the shim's), so it is
// never trusted: it must itself be inside the environment. Paths are resolved
// through links where they exist, and compared without case on Windows. The
// answer is the reason of the 🔴 question, in both languages.
func (d *Daemon) checkPCWrites(cwd string, ws []classify.PCWrite) classify.Text {
	for _, w := range ws {
		if why := d.checkPCWrite(cwd, w); !why.Empty() {
			return why
		}
	}
	return classify.Text{}
}

func (d *Daemon) checkPCWrite(cwd string, w classify.PCWrite) classify.Text {
	same, cat := classify.Same, classify.Cat
	if !w.Reason.Empty() {
		return cat(same(w.Cmd+" "), w.Reason)
	}
	if d.o.Workspace == "" {
		return cat(same(w.Cmd), classify.Text{EN: " writes on the PC and the agent environment is unknown", ES: " escribe en el PC y no se conoce el entorno del agente"})
	}
	root, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(d.o.Workspace)
	if err != nil {
		return cat(same(w.Cmd), classify.Text{EN: " writes on the PC and the agent environment cannot be read: ", ES: " escribe en el PC y no se puede leer el entorno del agente: "}, same(err.Error()))
	}
	outside := cat(same(w.Cmd), classify.Text{EN: " writes outside the agent environment: ", ES: " escribe fuera del entorno del agente: "})
	own := cat(same(w.Cmd), classify.Text{EN: " writes over the agent environment's own files: ", ES: " escribe sobre los ficheros propios del entorno del agente: "})
	if cwd == "" || !filepath.IsAbs(cwd) {
		return cat(outside, classify.Text{EN: "(unknown working directory " + cwd + ")", ES: "(directorio de trabajo desconocido " + cwd + ")"})
	}
	// adb runs in cwd as sent, and Linux and macOS resolve "out/.." on disk
	// (in the parent of out's target) while filepath.Clean just drops it.
	if cwd != filepath.Clean(cwd) || hasDotDot(cwd) {
		return cat(outside, classify.Text{EN: "(working directory not in clean form " + cwd + ")", ES: "(el directorio de trabajo no está en forma limpia: " + cwd + ")"})
	}
	for _, t := range append([]string{"."}, w.Targets...) {
```
